`CHEATSHEET.md`:

```md
# pymew cheatsheet

```python
import pymew as pm
```

## Memory

```python
# target: process name or pid
p = pm.mem.open(target, debug=False)  # -> Process
p.pid()                               # -> int
p.name()                              # -> str
p.path()                              # -> str
p.alive()                             # -> bool
p.is_64bit()                          # -> bool
p.base(module="")                     # -> int
p.modules()                           # -> list[Module] .name .base .end .size
p.module(name)                        # -> Module
p.regions()                           # -> list[Region] .start .end .size .protection
p.refresh()                           # -> None
p.set_debug(True)                     # -> None
p.close()                             # -> None

pm.mem.processes()                    # -> list[ProcessEntry] .pid .name
pm.mem.exists(target)                 # -> bool
pm.mem.pid(target)                    # -> int
pm.mem.name(target)                   # -> str
pm.mem.path(target)                   # -> str
pm.mem.is_64bit(target)               # -> bool
```

### Types

```python
pm.mem.i8
pm.mem.i16
pm.mem.i32
pm.mem.i64
pm.mem.u8
pm.mem.u16
pm.mem.u32
pm.mem.u64
pm.mem.f32
pm.mem.f64
pm.mem.bool
pm.mem.ptr
pm.mem.vec2
pm.mem.vec3

pm.mem.string(size)                  # -> Type    UTF-8 buffer
pm.mem.wstring(size)                 # -> Type    UTF-16 buffer
pm.mem.fstring(char_size=2)          # -> Type    Unreal FString
pm.mem.bytes(size)                   # -> Type
pm.mem.array(elem, count, stride=0)  # -> Type    fixed array / struct list
```

### Read / write

```python
p.read(address, typeDesc, count=0)       # -> Any    depends on typeDesc
p.read(address, pm.mem.i32)              # -> int
p.read(address, pm.mem.i32, count=16)    # -> list[int]
p.read(address, pm.mem.string(32))       # -> str
p.read(address, pm.mem.bytes(16))        # -> bytes    raw, not list[int]
p.read(address, pm.mem.f32, count=3)     # -> list[float]
p.read(address, Player)                  # -> Player

p.write(address, value)                  # -> int    bytes written
p.write(address, pm.mem.i32(5))          # -> int
p.write(address, pm.mem.i32([1, 2, 3]))  # -> int
p.write(address, pm.mem.wstring("hi"))   # -> int
p.write(address, pm.mem.string("hi"))    # -> int
p.write(address, pm.mem.bytes(b"\x90"))  # -> int

# read() takes a type (pm.mem.i32)
# write() takes a value (pm.mem.i32(5))
```

### Structs

```python
class Weapon(pm.mem.Struct):
    id = pm.mem.i32.at(0x00)
    ammo = pm.mem.i16.at(0x04)

class Player(pm.mem.Struct):
    pos = pm.mem.vec3.at(0x08)
    health = pm.mem.i32.at(0x100)
    name = pm.mem.string(32).at(0x219)
    weapon = Weapon.at(0x2A0)

pl = p.read(address, Player)                           # -> Player
pl.health                                              # -> int
pl.name                                                # -> str
pl.pos.x                                               # -> float
pl.weapon.ammo                                         # -> int

pl.health = 100
p.write(address, pl)                                   # -> int

pl.address(field="")                                   # -> int
Player.offset(field)                                   # -> int
Player.size()                                          # -> int
Player(health=100, pos=pm.vec3(1, 2, 3))               # -> Player

class Loadout(pm.mem.Struct):
    slots = pm.mem.array(Weapon, 3).at(0x10)

p.read(address, pm.mem.array(Weapon, 2))               # -> list[Weapon]
p.read(address, pm.mem.array(Weapon, 2, stride=0x20))  # -> list[Weapon]
lo = p.read(address, Loadout)                          # -> Loadout
lo.slots                                               # -> list[Weapon]
```

### Pointers / scan

```python
p.chain(base, offsets)                    # -> int
p.read_chain(base, offsets, typeDesc)     # -> Any    depends on typeDesc
p.resolve_rip(address, offset=3, size=7)  # -> int
p.scan(pattern, module="", first=False)   # -> list[int] | int | None
p.scan_range(pattern, start, stop)        # -> list[int]
pm.mem.scan_bytes(pattern, data)          # -> list[int]
```

## Overlay

```python
ov = pm.overlay.open(
    target=None,
    fps=0,
    title="pymew",
    exit_key=None,
    track=False,
    log_level=None,
    imgui_bundle=False,
    imgui_key=None,
    msaa=True
)  # -> Overlay

while ov.loop():                                                    # -> bool
    pm.draw.text("hi", 10, 10, 20, color=0xFFFFFFFF, align="left")  # -> None

ov.width()                                                          # -> int
ov.height()                                                         # -> int
ov.pos()                                                            # -> vec2
ov.set_pos(x, y)                                                    # -> None
ov.set_size(width, height)                                          # -> None
ov.set_title(title)                                                 # -> None
ov.monitor(index)                                                   # -> None
ov.clickthrough(True)                                               # -> None
ov.topmost(True)                                                    # -> None
ov.interact(True)                                                   # -> None
ov.fps()                                                            # -> int
ov.set_fps(fps)                                                     # -> None
ov.screenshot(path)                                                 # -> None
ov.imgui_active()                                                   # -> bool
ov.begin()                                                          # -> None
ov.end()                                                            # -> None
ov.close()                                                          # -> None

# loop() stays True until the exit key or window close
# interact() enters the input mode that imgui_key also toggles
# begin()/end() is only required for manual frame handling
```

### Text / fonts

```python
# align: left | center | right
# text: str or list[str], list entries become lines
d.text(
    text, x, y, size,
    color=0xFFFFFFFF,
    align="left",
    outline=False,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> None
d.text_ex(
    font, text, x, y, size,
    color=0xFFFFFFFF,
    spacing=1.0,
    align="left",
    outline=False,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> None
d.measure_text(text, size, spacing=1.0)  # -> vec2
d.measure_text_ex(font, text, size, spacing=1.0)  # -> vec2

# chars: latin | cyrillic | greek | all
d.load_font(path, size, chars="latin")                                          # -> Font
d.load_font_bytes(data, size, chars="latin", kind="ttf")                        # -> Font
d.fonts()                                                                       # -> list[str]

d.set_font(font=None)                                                           # -> None
d.font()                                                                        # -> Font | None
font.text(text, x, y, size=20.0, color=0xFFFFFFFF, align="left")                # -> None
font.measure(text, size=20.0, spacing=1.0)                                      # -> vec2
# filter: point | bilinear | trilinear
font.filter(filter="bilinear")                                                  # -> None
font.size()                                                                     # -> int
font.close()                                                                    # -> None

d.fps(
    x, y,
    color=0xFFFFFFFF,
    font=None,
    outline=False,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> None    defaults to active font
```

### Entity box

```python
# style: corner | line | filled
eb = d.entity_box(
    x, y, w, h,
    color=0xFFFFFFFF,
    style="line",
    fill=0.0,
    thick=1.5,
    outline=True,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> EntityBox
eb = d.entity_box_from_points(
    top, bottom,
    color=0xFFFFFFFF,
    ratio=0.85,
    style="line",
    fill=0.0,
    thick=1.5,
    outline=True,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> EntityBox

eb.set_box(x, y, w, h)  # -> None
eb.x                    # -> float
eb.y                    # -> float
eb.w                    # -> float
eb.h                    # -> float
eb.color                # -> int
eb.style                # -> str
eb.fill                 # -> float
eb.thick                # -> float
eb.outline              # -> bool
eb.outline_color        # -> int | None
eb.outline_thick        # -> float

# side: left | right | top | bottom
# align: left | center | right
# reverse flips the fill direction
# max: value at a full bar, value/max is the fill fraction (default 1.0)
eb.add_bar(
    side,
    color,
    value=1.0,
    thick=4.0,
    gap=6.0,
    segments=None,
    length=1.0,
    align="center",
    reverse=False,
    outline=True,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0,
    max=1.0
)  # -> None
# anchor: top-left | top | top-right | left | right | bottom-left | bottom | bottom-right
# text: str or list[str]
eb.add_text(
    anchor,
    text,
    color,
    size=12.0,
    font=None,
    gap=0.0,
    nudge=None,
    outline=True,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> None
# source: bottom | center | top
# style: solid | dashed | dotted
# spacing: gap in px
eb.add_snapline(
    to,
    color,
    source="bottom",
    style="solid",
    thick=1.0,
    spacing=0.0,
    outline=True,
    outline_color=None,
    outline_thick=1.0
)  # -> None

eb.draw()               # -> None
eb.clear()              # -> None
```

pm.input.key_down(value)                   # -> bool
pm.input.key_down("w")                     # -> bool
pm.input.key_down(87)                      # -> bool
pm.input.key_pressed(value)                # -> bool
pm.input.key_up(value)                     # -> bool
pm.input.mouse_pos()                       # -> vec2
# value: left | right | middle | x1 | x2 | 1..255
pm.input.mouse_down(value)                 # -> bool
pm.input.mouse_pressed(value)              # -> bool
pm.input.mouse_up(value)                   # -> bool
pm.input.mouse_buttons()                   # -> list[int]

v.closest(others)                 # -> vec2
v.to_tuple()                      # -> tuple[float, float]
```

<p align="center"><img src="assets/logo.png" alt="pymew" width="220"></p>
<h3 align="center">A Python memory manipulation and overlay library designed to simplify the development of external game cheats</h3>

## Cheatsheet / Features

An overview of the full API can be found in the **[cheatsheet](CHEATSHEET.md)** — memory, overlay, drawing, input, hotkeys and more...

A type stub `pymew.pyi` ships with the package, so autocompletion and `mypy` work out of the box.

## Installation

```bash
pip install pymew
```

Requires 64-bit Python 3.7 or newer.

## Quick start

```python
import pymew as pm

with pm.mem.open("game.exe") as process:
    with pm.overlay.open(target="game.exe", exit_key="end") as overlay:
        address = process.base() + 0x2A0

        while overlay.loop():
            health = process.read(address, pm.mem.i32)
            pm.draw.text(text=f"health: {health}", x=20, y=20, size=20)
```

## Compiling from source

Install [Nim](https://nim-lang.org/install.html) and Git, then:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/qb-0/pymew
cd pymew
nimble install --depsOnly -y
nim c pymew
```

`examples/ac_cross_esp.py`:

```py
import pymew as pm


isWindows = pm.utils.os_name() == "windows"


class Options:
    process = "ac_client.exe" if isWindows else "linux_64_client"
    max_entities = 32
    fps = 144
    title = f"pymew cross platform esp"
    exit_key = "end"
    track = True

    color_enemy = pm.color.get("orange")
    color_team = pm.color.get("cyan")
    color_name = pm.color.get("white")
    color_outline = pm.color.get("black")
    color_outline_bar = pm.color.get("white")
    health_high = pm.color.get("lime")
    health_mid = pm.color.get("yellow")
    health_low = pm.color.get("red")
    health_mid_at = 50
    health_low_at = 25

    box_style = "corner"
    box_fill = 0.05
    box_ratio = 0.7
    box_thick = 1.5
    box_outline = True
    box_outline_thick = 1.0

    bar_side = "left"
    bar_thick = 4
    bar_gap = 6
    bar_reverse = False
    bar_outline_thick = 1.0

    font_name = "arial.ttf" if isWindows else "AdwaitaMono-Bold.ttf"
    font_size = 14

    name_anchor = "top"
    name_size = 14
    name_nudge = (0, -5)
    name_outline_thick = 2.0

    snapline_anchor = "bottom"
    snapline_source = "bottom"
    snapline_style = "dashed"
    snapline_spacing = 8.0
    snapline_outline_thick = 1.0

    show_fps = True


class Pointer:
    if isWindows:
        entity_list = 0x18AC04
        local_player = 0x18AC00
        view_matrix = 0x17DFD0
    else:
        entity_list = 0x1A3520
        local_player = 0x1A3518
        view_matrix = 0x1B4FCC


class Offsets:
    if isWindows:
        name = 0x205
        health = 0xEC
        team = 0x30C
        pos = 0x04
        feet = 0x50
        head = 0x58
    else:
        name = 0x219
        health = 0x100
        team = 0x320
        pos = 0x08
        feet = 0x54
        head = 0x5C


class Entity(pm.mem.Struct):
    pos = pm.mem.vec3.at(Offsets.pos)
    feet = pm.mem.f32.at(Offsets.feet)
    head = pm.mem.f32.at(Offsets.head)
    health = pm.mem.i32.at(Offsets.health)
    name = pm.mem.string(30).at(Offsets.name)
    team = pm.mem.i32.at(Offsets.team)

    view = None

    @property
    def alive(self):
        return self.health > 0 and self.health <= 100

    @property
    def feetpos(self):
        return self.project(self.pos.z - self.feet)

    @property
    def headpos(self):
        return self.project(self.pos.z + self.head)

    def project(self, z):
        matrix, width, height = self.view
        x, y = self.pos.x, self.pos.y
        clip_w = x * matrix[3] + y * matrix[7] + z * matrix[11] + matrix[15]
        if clip_w < 0.2:
            return None
        clip_x = x * matrix[0] + y * matrix[4] + z * matrix[8] + matrix[12]
        clip_y = x * matrix[1] + y * matrix[5] + z * matrix[9] + matrix[13]
        return pm.vec2(
            width * 0.5 * (1.0 + clip_x / clip_w),
            height * 0.5 * (1.0 - clip_y / clip_w),
        )


def main():
    if not pm.mem.exists(Options.process):
        print(f"{Options.process} is not running")
        return

    d = pm.draw

    with pm.mem.open(Options.process) as p:
        base = p.base()
        pointer_size = 8 if p.is_64bit() else 4
        with pm.overlay.open(
            target=Options.process,
            fps=Options.fps,
            title=Options.title,
            exit_key=Options.exit_key,
            track=Options.track,
        ) as ov:
            try:
                font = d.load_font(Options.font_name, Options.font_size)
            except pm.errors.Error:
                print(f"font '{Options.font_name}' not found, using the default font")
                font = None
            d.set_font(font)

            while ov.loop():
                if not p.alive():
                    break
                width = ov.width()
                height = ov.height()
                matrix = p.read(base + Pointer.view_matrix, pm.mem.f32, 16)
                entity_list = p.read(base + Pointer.entity_list, pm.mem.ptr)
                local_addr = p.read(base + Pointer.local_player, pm.mem.ptr)
                local_player = p.read(local_addr, Entity) if local_addr else None

                if Options.show_fps:
                    d.fps(10, 10)

                if not entity_list:
                    continue

                for i in range(Options.max_entities):
                    addr = p.read(entity_list + i * pointer_size, pm.mem.ptr)
                    if not addr or addr == local_addr:
                        continue

                    try:
                        ent = p.read(addr, Entity)
                    except (pm.errors.Error, UnicodeDecodeError):
                        continue

                    if not ent.alive:
                        continue

                    ent.view = (matrix, width, height)
                    feet = ent.feetpos
                    top = ent.headpos
                    if feet is None or top is None:
                        continue

                    friendly = local_player.team in (0, 1) and ent.team == local_player.team
                    color = Options.color_team if friendly else Options.color_enemy

                    if ent.health <= Options.health_low_at:
                        bar_color = Options.health_low
                    elif ent.health <= Options.health_mid_at:
                        bar_color = Options.health_mid
                    else:
                        bar_color = Options.health_high

                    box = d.entity_box_from_points(
                        top, feet, color,
                        ratio=Options.box_ratio,
                        style=Options.box_style,
                        fill=Options.box_fill,
                        thick=Options.box_thick,
                        outline=Options.box_outline,
                        outline_color=Options.color_outline,
                        outline_thick=Options.box_outline_thick,
                    )
                    box.add_bar(
                        Options.bar_side, bar_color,
                        value=ent.health / 100.0,
                        thick=Options.bar_thick,
                        gap=Options.bar_gap,
                        reverse=Options.bar_reverse,
                        outline_color=Options.color_outline_bar,
                        outline_thick=Options.bar_outline_thick,
                    )
                    box.add_text(
                        Options.name_anchor, ent.name or "player", Options.color_name,
                        size=Options.name_size,
                        font=font,
                        nudge=Options.name_nudge,
                        outline_color=Options.color_outline,
                        outline_thick=Options.name_outline_thick,
                    )
                    box.add_text(
                        "bottom",
                        [f"{ent.health} HP", f"{int(ent.pos.distance(local_player.pos))}m"],
                        Options.color_name,
                        size=Options.name_size,
                        font=font,
                        nudge=(0, 5),
                        outline_color=Options.color_outline,
                        outline_thick=Options.name_outline_thick,
                    )
                    box.add_snapline(
                        Options.snapline_anchor, pm.color.fade(color, 0.3),
                        source=Options.snapline_source,
                        style=Options.snapline_style,
                        spacing=Options.snapline_spacing,
                        outline_thick=Options.snapline_outline_thick,
                    )
                    box.draw()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

`examples/imgui.py`:

```py
import pymew as pm
from imgui_bundle import imgui


def main():
    text = ""
    draw_box = True

    with pm.overlay.open(
        fps=144,
        title="pymew + imgui",
        exit_key="end",
        imgui_bundle=True,
        imgui_key="insert",
    ) as ov:
        while ov.loop():
            if ov.imgui_active():
                imgui.begin("pymew + imgui")
                _, draw_box = imgui.checkbox("draw box", draw_box)
                _, text = imgui.input_text("text", text)
                imgui.text(f"{ov.fps()} fps")
                imgui.end()

            if draw_box:
                box = pm.draw.entity_box_from_points(
                    (ov.width() * 0.50, ov.height() * 0.25),
                    (ov.width() * 0.60, ov.height() * 0.65),
                    pm.color.get("orange"),
                    ratio=0.5,
                    style="corner",
                    thick=2.0,
                )
                box.add_text("top", text, size=14)
                box.draw()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

`examples/overlay_test.py`:

```py
import math
import struct
import zlib

import pymew as pm

try:
    from imgui_bundle import imgui
except ImportError:
    print("imgui_bundle not found, imgui will be disabled")
    print("install imgui_bundle with `pip install imgui_bundle`")
    imgui = None

d = pm.draw

VIRTUAL_WIDTH = 1920
VIRTUAL_HEIGHT = 1080
GRID_COLUMNS = 5
GRID_LEFT = 40
GRID_TOP = 108
CELL_WIDTH = 240
CELL_HEIGHT = 140
RIGHT_COLUMN = 1280

WHITE = pm.color.get("white")
MUTED = pm.color.get("silver")
FAINT = pm.color.get("gray")
BLACK = pm.color.get("black")
ORANGE = pm.color.get("orange")
CYAN = pm.color.get("cyan")
LIME = pm.color.get("lime")
PINK = pm.color.get("hotpink")
YELLOW = pm.color.get("yellow")
SKY = pm.color.get("deepskyblue")
VIOLET = pm.color.get("mediumpurple")
RED = pm.color.get("tomato")


def halo(sheet, on):
    return {
        "outline": on and sheet.outlines,
        "outline_color": BLACK,
        "outline_thick": sheet.unit(sheet.outline_thick),
    }


def demo_line(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.line(
        cx - u(42),
        cy - u(24),
        cx + u(42),
        cy + u(24),
        color,
        thick=u(4),
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_polyline(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.polyline(
        (
            (cx - u(44), cy + u(20)),
            (cx - u(15), cy - u(22)),
            (cx + u(15), cy + u(18)),
            (cx + u(44), cy - u(16)),
        ),
        color,
        thick=u(4),
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_rect(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.rect(cx - u(45), cy - u(28), u(90), u(56), color, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_rect_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.rect(
        cx - u(45), cy - u(28), u(90), u(56), color, fill=False, thick=u(3), **halo(sheet, on)
    )


def demo_rect_round(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.rect(cx - u(45), cy - u(28), u(90), u(56), color, round=0.5, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_rect_gradient(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.rect_gradient(cx - u(45), cy - u(28), u(90), u(56), ORANGE, PINK, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_rect_rotated(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.rect(
        cx - u(45),
        cy - u(28),
        u(90),
        u(56),
        color,
        round=0.25,
        rotation=time * 30.0,
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_circle(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.circle(cx, cy, sheet.unit(30), color, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_circle_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.circle(cx, cy, sheet.unit(30), color, fill=False, thick=sheet.unit(3), **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_sector(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    start = 30.0 + 25.0 * math.sin(time * 0.9)
    d.sector(cx, cy, sheet.unit(32), start, start + 190.0, color, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_sector_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    start = 20.0 + 25.0 * math.sin(time * 0.9)
    d.sector(
        cx,
        cy,
        sheet.unit(32),
        start,
        start + 190.0,
        color,
        fill=False,
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_ring(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.ring(cx, cy, sheet.unit(15), sheet.unit(32), color, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_ring_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.ring(
        cx,
        cy,
        sheet.unit(15),
        sheet.unit(32),
        color,
        fill=False,
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_ring_arc(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    start = 20.0 + 40.0 * math.sin(time * 0.7)
    d.ring(
        cx,
        cy,
        sheet.unit(15),
        sheet.unit(32),
        color,
        start=start,
        end=start + 250.0,
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_ellipse(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.ellipse(cx, cy, sheet.unit(38), sheet.unit(22), color, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_ellipse_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.ellipse(cx, cy, u(38), u(22), color, fill=False, thick=u(3), **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_triangle(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.triangle(
        cx,
        cy - u(32),
        cx - u(40),
        cy + u(28),
        cx + u(40),
        cy + u(24),
        color,
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_triangle_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    u = sheet.unit
    d.triangle(
        cx,
        cy - u(32),
        cx - u(40),
        cy + u(28),
        cx + u(40),
        cy + u(24),
        color,
        fill=False,
        thick=u(3),
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


def demo_poly(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.poly(cx, cy, 6, sheet.unit(32), color, rotation=time * 40.0, **halo(sheet, on))


def demo_poly_stroke(sheet, cx, cy, on, color, time):
    d.poly(
        cx,
        cy,
        6,
        sheet.unit(32),
        color,
        rotation=time * 40.0,
        fill=False,
        thick=sheet.unit(3),
        **halo(sheet, on),
    )


DEMOS = (
    ("line", demo_line, ORANGE),
    ("polyline", demo_polyline, SKY),
    ("rect", demo_rect, LIME),
    ("rect stroke", demo_rect_stroke, ORANGE),
    ("rect round", demo_rect_round, VIOLET),
    ("rect gradient", demo_rect_gradient, WHITE),
    ("circle", demo_circle, CYAN),
    ("circle stroke", demo_circle_stroke, PINK),
    ("sector", demo_sector, YELLOW),
    ("sector stroke", demo_sector_stroke, LIME),
    ("ring", demo_ring, ORANGE),
    ("ring stroke", demo_ring_stroke, SKY),
    ("ring arc", demo_ring_arc, VIOLET),
    ("ellipse", demo_ellipse, CYAN),
    ("ellipse stroke", demo_ellipse_stroke, PINK),
    ("triangle", demo_triangle, YELLOW),
    ("triangle stroke", demo_triangle_stroke, ORANGE),
    ("poly", demo_poly, LIME),
    ("poly stroke", demo_poly_stroke, SKY),
    ("rotated rect", demo_rect_rotated, VIOLET),
)


def checker_png(size=64, cell=8):
    rows = bytearray()
    for y in range(size):
        rows.append(0)
        for x in range(size):
            value = 235 if (x // cell + y // cell) % 2 else 45
            rows.extend((value, value // 2, 255 - value, 255))

    def chunk(tag, payload):
        return (
            struct.pack(">I", len(payload))
            + tag
            + payload
            + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(tag + payload))
        )

    header = struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", size, size, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0)
    return (
        b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
        + chunk(b"IHDR", header)
        + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(bytes(rows)))
        + chunk(b"IEND", b"")
    )


def health_color(value):
    if value > 0.6:
        return LIME
    if value > 0.3:
        return YELLOW
    return RED


class Sheet:
    def __init__(self, ov):
        self.ov = ov
        self.font = self.load_font()
        self.texture = d.load_texture_bytes("png", checker_png())
        d.set_font(self.font)
        self.scale = 1.0
        self.origin_x = 0.0
        self.origin_y = 0.0
        self.time = 0.0
        self.outlines = True
        self.outline_thick = 2.0
        self.animate = True

    @staticmethod
    def load_font():
        name = "arial.ttf" if pm.utils.os_name() == "windows" else "AdwaitaMono-Bold.ttf"
        try:
            return d.load_font(name, 32)
        except pm.errors.Error:
            return None

    def close(self):
        d.set_font(None)
        self.texture.close()
        if self.font is not None:
            self.font.close()

    def unit(self, value):
        return value * self.scale

    def x(self, value):
        return self.origin_x + value * self.scale

    def y(self, value):
        return self.origin_y + value * self.scale

    def text(self, value, x, y, size, color, align="left"):
        d.text(
            value,
            self.x(x),
            self.y(y),
            self.unit(size),
            color=color,
            align=align,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(2),
        )

    def frame(self):
        if self.animate:
            self.time = pm.utils.time()
        width, height = self.ov.width(), self.ov.height()
        self.scale = min(width / VIRTUAL_WIDTH, height / VIRTUAL_HEIGHT)
        self.origin_x = (width - VIRTUAL_WIDTH * self.scale) / 2
        self.origin_y = (height - VIRTUAL_HEIGHT * self.scale) / 2
        self.header()
        self.shapes(self.time)
        self.texts()
        self.textures(self.time)
        self.boxes(self.time)
        self.menu()

    def menu(self):
        if imgui is None or not self.ov.imgui_active():
            return
        imgui.set_next_window_pos(
            (self.x(40), self.y(VIRTUAL_HEIGHT - 40)),
            imgui.Cond_.first_use_ever,
            (0.0, 1.0),
        )
        imgui.set_next_window_size((300, 112), imgui.Cond_.always)
        imgui.begin("controls")
        _, self.outlines = imgui.checkbox("shape outlines", self.outlines)
        _, self.outline_thick = imgui.slider_float(
            "shape outline width", self.outline_thick, 0.5, 5.0, "%.1f"
        )
        _, self.animate = imgui.checkbox("animate", self.animate)
        imgui.end()

    def header(self):
        self.text("pymew overlay test", 40, 18, 28, WHITE)
        label = str(self.ov.fps())
        width = d.measure_text_ex(self.font, label, 20.0).x
        d.fps(
            self.x(1880) - width,
            self.y(20),
            color=LIME,
            font=self.font,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=2.0,
        )

    def shapes(self, time):
        for index, (label, painter, color) in enumerate(DEMOS):
            row, column = divmod(index, GRID_COLUMNS)
            x = GRID_LEFT + column * CELL_WIDTH
            y = GRID_TOP + row * CELL_HEIGHT
            self.text(label, x, y, 15, MUTED)
            for offset, on, caption in ((80, False, "plain"), (190, True, "outline")):
                painter(self, self.x(x + offset), self.y(y + 70), on, color, time)
                self.text(caption, x + offset, y + 120, 12, FAINT, align="center")

    def texts(self):
        x = RIGHT_COLUMN
        self.text("text", x, 108, 18, WHITE)
        d.text("plain text", self.x(x), self.y(136), self.unit(16), MUTED)
        d.text(
            "outlined",
            self.x(x + 150),
            self.y(136),
            self.unit(16),
            ORANGE,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(3),
        )
        d.text(
            "thick outline",
            self.x(x),
            self.y(166),
            self.unit(16),
            CYAN,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(4),
        )
        d.text(
            "right aligned",
            self.x(x + 600),
            self.y(166),
            self.unit(16),
            PINK,
            align="right",
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(2),
        )
        d.text(
            ["list entries", "become lines"],
            self.x(x),
            self.y(198),
            self.unit(16),
            SKY,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(1.5),
        )
        d.text_ex(
            self.font,
            "text_ex",
            self.x(x + 300),
            self.y(198),
            self.unit(16),
            LIME,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(2),
        )

    def textures(self, time):
        x = RIGHT_COLUMN
        y = 298
        self.text("texture", x, 268, 18, WHITE)
        scale = self.unit(1.5)
        size = self.texture.width() * scale
        d.texture(self.texture, self.x(x), self.y(y), scale=scale)
        d.texture(
            self.texture,
            self.x(x + 150),
            self.y(y),
            scale=scale,
            rotation=time * 40.0,
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(2),
        )
        d.texture_rect(
            self.texture,
            (0, 0, self.texture.width(), self.texture.height()),
            (self.x(x + 300), self.y(y), size, size),
            outline=True,
            outline_color=BLACK,
            outline_thick=self.unit(2),
        )
        self.text("plain", x, y + 106, 12, FAINT)
        self.text("rotated", x + 150, y + 106, 12, FAINT)
        self.text("texture_rect", x + 300, y + 106, 12, FAINT)

    def boxes(self, time):
        x = RIGHT_COLUMN
        top = 500
        width = 120
        height = 300
        spacing = 180
        left = x + (600 - width - spacing * 2) / 2
        self.text("entity box", x, 446, 18, WHITE)
        wave = (math.sin(time * 1.3) + 1.0) / 2.0
        specs = (
            ("line", ORANGE, wave, "dashed"),
            ("corner", CYAN, 1.0 - wave, "solid"),
            ("filled", pm.color.fade(LIME, 0.35), 0.35 + 0.65 * wave, "dotted"),
        )
        for index, (style, color, value, snapline) in enumerate(specs):
            box_left = left + index * spacing
            common = {
                "color": color,
                "style": style,
                "thick": self.unit(2),
                "outline": True,
                "outline_color": BLACK,
                "outline_thick": self.unit(1.5),
            }
            if style == "corner":
                box_center = box_left + width / 2
                box = d.entity_box_from_points(
                    (self.x(box_center), self.y(top)),
                    (self.x(box_center), self.y(top + height)),
                    ratio=width / height,
                    **common,
                )
            else:
                box = d.entity_box(
                    self.x(box_left),
                    self.y(top),
                    self.unit(width),
                    self.unit(height),
                    **common,
                )
            box.add_bar(
                "left",
                health_color(value),
                value=value,
                thick=self.unit(4),
                gap=self.unit(5),
                outline=True,
                outline_color=BLACK,
                outline_thick=self.unit(1),
            )
            box.add_bar(
                "right",
                SKY,
                value=1.0 - value,
                thick=self.unit(4),
                gap=self.unit(5),
                reverse=True,
            )
            box.add_text(
                "top",
                style,
                WHITE,
                size=self.unit(13),
                outline=True,
                outline_color=BLACK,
                outline_thick=self.unit(2),
            )
            box.add_snapline(
                "bottom",
                WHITE,
                source="bottom",
                style=snapline,
                thick=self.unit(1.5),
                spacing=self.unit(8),
                outline=True,
                outline_color=BLACK,
                outline_thick=self.unit(1),
            )
            box.draw()


def main():
    with pm.overlay.open(
        fps=144,
        title="pymew overlay test",
        exit_key="end",
        imgui_bundle=imgui is not None,
        imgui_key="insert" if imgui else None,
    ) as ov:
        sheet = Sheet(ov)
        if imgui:
            ov.interact(True)
        try:
            while ov.loop():
                sheet.frame()
        finally:
            sheet.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

    ("vec3", "vec3")
  ]

  utilsFunctionExports = [
    ("os_name", "_utilsOsName"),
    ("screen_size", "_utilsScreenSize"),
    ("monitors", "_utilsMonitors"),
    ("window_info", "_utilsWindowInfo"),
    ("windows", "_utilsWindows"),
    ("time", "_utilsTime"),
    ("set_log_level", "_utilsSetLogLevel")
  ]

  drawFunctionExports = [
    ("point", "draw_point"),
    ("line", "draw_line"),
    ("polyline", "draw_polyline"),
    ("rect", "draw_rect"),
    ("rect_gradient", "draw_rect_gradient"),
    ("circle", "draw_circle"),
    ("sector", "draw_sector"),
    ("ring", "draw_ring"),
    ("ellipse", "draw_ellipse"),
    ("triangle", "draw_triangle"),
    ("poly", "draw_poly"),
    ("text", "draw_text"),
    ("text_ex", "draw_text_ex"),
    ("measure_text", "draw_measure_text"),
    ("measure_text_ex", "draw_measure_text_ex"),
    ("set_font", "draw_set_font"),
    ("font", "draw_font"),
    ("fps", "draw_fps"),
    ("load_texture", "draw_load_texture"),
    ("load_texture_bytes", "draw_load_texture_bytes"),
    ("texture", "draw_texture"),
    ("texture_rect", "draw_texture_rect"),
    ("load_font", "draw_load_font"),
    ("load_font_bytes", "draw_load_font_bytes"),
    ("fonts", "draw_fonts"),
    ("screenshot", "draw_screenshot"),
    ("entity_box", "draw_entity_box"),
    ("entity_box_from_points", "draw_entity_box_from_points")
  ]

proc moduleObj(): PyObject =
  pyImport("pymew")

proc cacheNamespace(name: string, ns: PyObject): PPyObject =
  setAttr(moduleObj(), name, ns)
  result = nimValueToPy(ns)

proc buildMemNamespace(): PyObject =
  ensureTypes()
  let
    ns = newNamespace()
    m = moduleObj()
  for (key, exported) in memFunctionExports:
    setAttr(ns, key.cstring, getAttr(m, exported.cstring))
  for (key, desc) in [
    ("i8", descI8), ("i16", descI16), ("i32", descI32), ("i64", descI64),
    ("u8", descU8), ("u16", descU16), ("u32", descU32), ("u64", descU64),
    ("f32", descF32), ("f64", descF64), ("bool", descBool), ("ptr", descPtr),
    ("vec2", descVec2), ("vec3", descVec3)
  ]:
    setAttr(ns, key.cstring, desc)
  setAttr(ns, "Struct", structClass)
  result = ns

proc buildErrorsNamespace(): PyObject =
  ensureTypes()
  let ns = newNamespace()
  for (name, cls) in [
    ("Error", errorClass),
    ("ProcessNotFound", processNotFoundClass),
    ("ModuleNotFound", moduleNotFoundClass),
    ("AccessError", accessErrorClass),
    ("PermissionDenied", permissionDeniedClass),
    ("UnsupportedError", unsupportedClass),
    ("HotkeyError", hotkeyErrorClass),
    ("OverlayError", overlayErrorClass),
    ("WindowNotFound", windowNotFoundClass)
  ]:
    setAttr(ns, name.cstring, cls)
  result = ns

proc functionNamespace(exports: openArray[(string, string)]): PyObject =
  let
    ns = newNamespace()
    m = moduleObj()
  for (key, exported) in exports:
    setAttr(ns, key.cstring, getAttr(m, exported.cstring))
  result = ns

proc buildUtilsNamespace(): PyObject =
  ensureUtilsTypes()
  let ns = functionNamespace(utilsFunctionExports)
  setAttr(ns, "Monitor", monitorClass)
  setAttr(ns, "Window", windowClass)
  result = ns

proc buildDrawNamespace(): PyObject =
  ensureDrawTypes()
  let ns = functionNamespace(drawFunctionExports)
  setAttr(ns, "Font", fontClass)
  setAttr(ns, "Texture", textureClass)
  setAttr(ns, "EntityBox", entityBoxClass)
  result = ns

proc pyGetattr(name: string): PPyObject =
  case name
  of "__version__":
    nimValueToPy(pymewVersion)
  of "mem":
    cacheNamespace("mem", buildMemNamespace())
  of "vec":
    cacheNamespace("vec", functionNamespace(vecFunctionExports))
  of "errors":
    cacheNamespace("errors", buildErrorsNamespace())
  of "color":
    cacheNamespace("color", functionNamespace(colorFunctionExports))
  of "keys":
    cacheNamespace("keys", functionNamespace(keysFunctionExports))
  of "input":
    cacheNamespace("input", functionNamespace(inputFunctionExports))
  of "hotkey":
    cacheNamespace("hotkey", functionNamespace(hotkeyFunctionExports))
  of "overlay":
    cacheNamespace("overlay", functionNamespace(overlayFunctionExports))
  of "utils":
    cacheNamespace("utils", buildUtilsNamespace())
  of "draw":
    cacheNamespace("draw", buildDrawNamespace())
  else:
    setAttributeError("module 'pymew' has no attribute '" & name & "'")
    nil

exportAs(pyGetattr, "__getattr__")

```

`pymew.nim.cfg`:

```cfg
-d:release
--opt:speed
--app:lib
--cpu:amd64
--threads:on
--mm:arc
--hints:off

@if windows:
  --out:"pymew.pyd"
  --tlsEmulation:off
  --passC:"-fvisibility=hidden"
  --passL:"-static-libgcc"
@elif linux:
  --out:"pymew.so"
  --passC:"-fvisibility=hidden -Wno-int-conversion"
@end
```

`pymew.nimble`:

```nimble
version       = "0.1.0"
author        = "pymew"
description   = "A Python memory manipulation and overlay library designed to simplify the development of external game cheats"
license       = "MIT"
srcDir        = "."

requires "nimpy >= 0.2.1", "naylib == 26.08.0"

when defined(windows):
  requires "winim >= 4.0.0"
  bin = @["pymew.pyd"]
else:
  requires "x11 >= 1.2"
  bin = @["pymew.so"]

```

    def filter(self, filter: str = "bilinear") -> None: ...

    def close(self) -> None: ...
