```nim
proc drawSetFont*(font: PyObject = nil): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_set_font".} =
  pyWrap:
    if isNone(font):
      currentFont = nil
    else:
      discard fontData(font)
      currentFont = font
    nil

proc drawFont*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_font".} =
  pyWrap:
    if currentFont.isNil:
      newPyNone()
    else:
      nimValueToPy(currentFont)

proc drawFps*(
  x, y: float,
  color: int = DefaultColor,
  font: PyObject = nil,
  outline: bool = false,
  outline_color: PyObject = nil,
  outline_thick: float = 1.0
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_fps".} =
  pyWrap:
    checkOverlay("draw_fps")
    let
      p = if isNone(font): fontData(currentFont) else: fontData(font)
      label = $int(rl.getFPS())
      border = max(outline_thick, 0.0).float32
      outlineColor = resolveOutline(outline_color, color)
    drawOutlinedText(
      p,
      label,
      x.float32,
      y.float32,
      20.0,
      1.0,
      color,
      outlineColor,
      border,
      outlineActive(outline, border, outlineColor),
      taLeft
    )

proc drawLoadTexture*(path: string): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_load_texture".} =
  pyWrap:
    loadTextureFile(path)

proc drawLoadTextureBytes*(
  kind: string,
  data: PyObject
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_load_texture_bytes".} =
  pyWrap:
    loadTextureBuffer(kind, data)

proc drawTexture*(
  texture: PyObject,
  x, y: float,
  scale: float = 1.0,
  rotation: float = 0.0,
  tint: int = DefaultColor,
  opacity: float = 1.0,
  outline: bool = false,
  outline_color: PyObject = nil,
  outline_thick: float = 1.0
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_texture".} =
  pyWrap:
    checkOverlay("draw_texture")
    let
      p = textureData(texture)
      blended = withAlpha(tint, opacity)
      scaleFactor = max(scale, 0.0).float32
      angle = rotation.float32
      border = max(outline_thick, 0.0).float32
      outlineColor = resolveOutline(outline_color, tint)
    if outlineActive(outline, border, outlineColor):
      let
        origin = rl.Vector2(x: x.float32, y: y.float32)
        bounds = rl.Rectangle(
          x: origin.x,
          y: origin.y,
          width: float32(p[].width) * scaleFactor,
          height: float32(p[].height) * scaleFactor
        )
        corners = rotatedCorners(expanded(bounds, border / 2), angle, origin)
      for i in 0 ..< 4:
        rl.drawLine(
          corners[i],
          corners[(i + 1) mod 4],
          border,
          rl.getColor(uint32(outlineColor))
        )
    if alphaOf(blended) > 0:
      rl.drawTexture(
        p[],
        rl.Vector2(x: x.float32, y: y.float32),
        angle,
        scaleFactor,
        rl.getColor(uint32(blended))
      )

proc drawTextureRect*(
  texture: PyObject,
  source: PyObject,
  dest: PyObject,
  tint: int = DefaultColor,
  opacity: float = 1.0,
  outline: bool = false,
  outline_color: PyObject = nil,
  outline_thick: float = 1.0
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_texture_rect".} =
  pyWrap:
    checkOverlay("draw_texture_rect")
    let
      p = textureData(texture)
      src = rectOf(source)
      dst = rectOf(dest)
      blended = withAlpha(tint, opacity)
      border = max(outline_thick, 0.0).float32
      outlineColor = resolveOutline(outline_color, tint)
    if outlineActive(outline, border, outlineColor):
      rectBorder(dst, border, 0.0, 0, 0.0, rl.getColor(uint32(outlineColor)))
    if alphaOf(blended) > 0:
      rl.drawTexture(p[], src, dst, rl.Vector2(x: 0.0, y: 0.0), 0.0, rl.getColor(uint32(blended)))

proc drawLoadFont*(
  path: string,
  size: int,
  chars: string = "latin"
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_load_font".} =
  pyWrap:
    loadFontFile(path, size, chars)

proc drawLoadFontBytes*(
  data: PyObject,
  size: int,
  chars: string = "latin",
  kind: string = "ttf"
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_load_font_bytes".} =
  pyWrap:
    loadFontBuffer(data.to(seq[uint8]), size, chars, kind)

proc drawFonts*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_fonts".} =
  pyWrap:
    var names: seq[string]
    for path in systemFontFiles():
      names.add extractFilename(path)
    names.sort(proc(a, b: string): int = cmp(a.toLowerAscii(), b.toLowerAscii()))
    var
      items: seq[PPyObject]
      seen = initHashSet[string]()
    for name in names:
      if seen.containsOrIncl(name.toLowerAscii()):
        continue
      items.add nimValueToPy(name)
    newPyListTaking(items)

proc captureScreen*(path, op: string) =
  checkOverlay(op)
  if path.len == 0:
    raise newException(PmValueError, "screenshot path must not be empty")
  if not inFrame:
    raise newException(
      PmOverlayError,
      "screenshot must be called while a frame is open (between loop() and end())"
    )
  drawRenderBatchActive()
  let
    target = absolutePath(path)
    image = rl.loadImageFromScreen()
    saved = rl.exportImage(image, target)
  if not saved:
    raise newException(PmOverlayError, "cannot save screenshot to '" & path & "'")

proc drawScreenshot*(path: string): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_screenshot".} =
  pyWrap:
    captureScreen(path, "draw_screenshot")

proc drawEntityBox*(
  x, y, w, h: float,
  color: int = DefaultColor,
  style: string = "line",
  fill: float = 0.0,
  thick: float = 1.5,
  outline: bool = true,
  outline_color: PyObject = nil,
  outline_thick: float = 1.0
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_entity_box".} =
  pyWrap:
    makeEntityBox(x, y, w, h, color, style, fill, thick, outline, outline_color, outline_thick)

proc drawEntityBoxFromPoints*(
  top, bottom: PyObject,
  color: int = DefaultColor,
  ratio: float = 0.85,
  style: string = "line",
  fill: float = 0.0,
  thick: float = 1.5,
  outline: bool = true,
  outline_color: PyObject = nil,
  outline_thick: float = 1.0
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "draw_entity_box_from_points".} =
  pyWrap:
    let
      head = pointOf(top)
      feet = pointOf(bottom)
      height = max(feet.y - head.y, 0.0)
      width = height * (if ratio > 0: ratio else: 0.85).float32
      x = (head.x + feet.x) / 2 - width / 2
    makeEntityBox(
      float(x),
      float(head.y),
      float(width),
      float(height),
      color,
      style,
      fill,
      thick,
      outline,
      outline_color,
      outline_thick
    )
```

`src/errors.nim`:

```nim
import nimpy
import nimpy/py_lib as pyl

import pyutil
import platform/common

type
  PmError* = object of CatchableError
  PmProcessNotFound* = object of PmError
  PmModuleNotFound* = object of PmError
  PmAccessError* = object of PmError
  PmPermissionDenied* = object of PmAccessError
  PmUnsupported* = object of PmError
  PmBadType* = object of PmError
  PmValueError* = object of PmError
  PmHotkeyError* = object of PmError
  PmOverlayError* = object of PmError
  PmWindowNotFound* = object of PmError

var
  errorClass*: PyObject
  processNotFoundClass*: PyObject
  moduleNotFoundClass*: PyObject
  accessErrorClass*: PyObject
  permissionDeniedClass*: PyObject
  unsupportedClass*: PyObject
  hotkeyErrorClass*: PyObject
  overlayErrorClass*: PyObject
  windowNotFoundClass*: PyObject

proc initErrorClasses*() =
  if errorClass.isNil:
    errorClass = newClass("Error", getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "Exception"), pyDict())
    processNotFoundClass = newClassMulti(
      "ProcessNotFound", [errorClass, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "LookupError")], pyDict()
    )
    moduleNotFoundClass = newClassMulti(
      "ModuleNotFound", [errorClass, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "LookupError")], pyDict()
    )
    accessErrorClass = newClassMulti(
      "AccessError", [errorClass, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "OSError")], pyDict()
    )
    permissionDeniedClass = newClassMulti(
      "PermissionDenied", [accessErrorClass, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "PermissionError")], pyDict()
    )
    unsupportedClass = newClassMulti(
      "UnsupportedError", [errorClass, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "NotImplementedError")], pyDict()
    )
    hotkeyErrorClass = newClass("HotkeyError", errorClass, pyDict())
    overlayErrorClass = newClass("OverlayError", errorClass, pyDict())
    windowNotFoundClass = newClass("WindowNotFound", errorClass, pyDict())

proc setPyErr*(cls: PyObject, msg: string) =
  let target = if cls.isNil: getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "Exception") else: cls
  pyl.pyLib.PyErr_SetString(rawPy(target), cstring(msg))

proc setValueError*(msg: string) =
  pyl.pyLib.PyErr_SetString(pyl.pyLib.PyExc_ValueError, cstring(msg))

proc setTypeError*(msg: string) =
  pyl.pyLib.PyErr_SetString(pyl.pyLib.PyExc_TypeError, cstring(msg))

proc setAttributeError*(msg: string) =
  var cls {.global.}: PyObject
  if cls.isNil:
    cls = getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "AttributeError")
  pyl.pyLib.PyErr_SetString(rawPy(cls), cstring(msg))

proc setPyErrFor*(e: ref CatchableError) =
  if e of PmProcessNotFound:
    setPyErr(processNotFoundClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmModuleNotFound:
    setPyErr(moduleNotFoundClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmPermissionDenied:
    setPyErr(permissionDeniedClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmAccessError:
    setPyErr(accessErrorClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmUnsupported:
    setPyErr(unsupportedClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmBadType:
    setTypeError(e.msg)
  elif e of PmValueError:
    setValueError(e.msg)
  elif e of PmHotkeyError:
    setPyErr(hotkeyErrorClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmOverlayError:
    setPyErr(overlayErrorClass, e.msg)
  elif e of PmWindowNotFound:
    setPyErr(windowNotFoundClass, e.msg)
  elif e of ValueError:
    setValueError(e.msg)
  elif e of OsUnsupported:
    setPyErr(unsupportedClass, e.msg)
  elif e of LibraryError:
    setPyErr(unsupportedClass, e.msg)
  elif e of OsFailure:
    setPyErr(accessErrorClass, e.msg)
  else:
    setPyErr(errorClass, e.msg)

template pyWrap*(body: untyped) =
  try:
    initErrorClasses()
    result = pyResult(block: body)
  except CatchableError as e:
    setPyErrFor(e)
    result = nil

```

`src/hotkey.nim`:

```nim
import std/[strutils, tables]
import nimpy
import nimpy/py_types

import pyutil, errors, keys
import platform/api

pyExportModule("pymew")

initHotkeyState()

const
  maxHotkeyId = 0xBFFF

type
  HotkeyBinding = object
    id: int
    combo: string
    code: int
    mods: uint8
    callback: PyObject

var
  bindings: seq[HotkeyBinding]
  comboIndex: Table[string, int]
  idIndex: Table[int, int]
  nextId = 1
  pumping = false

proc comboArg(value: PyObject): string =
  if not pyIsInstance(value, "str"):
    raise newException(PmBadType, "hotkey combo must be a str")
  value.to(string)

proc isModifier(token: string): bool =
  token in ["ctrl", "alt", "shift", "super"]

proc comboString(mods: uint8, code: int): string =
  if (mods and hmCtrl) != 0:
    result.add "ctrl+"
  if (mods and hmAlt) != 0:
    result.add "alt+"
  if (mods and hmShift) != 0:
    result.add "shift+"
  if (mods and hmSuper) != 0:
    result.add "super+"
  result.add keyName(code)

proc parseCombo(text: string): tuple[combo: string, code: int, mods: uint8] =
  let stripped = text.strip()
  if stripped.len == 0:
    raise newException(PmValueError, "hotkey combo must not be empty")
  let parts = stripped.split('+')
  var mods: uint8
  for i, part in parts:
    let token = normalizeName(part)
    if token.len == 0:
      raise newException(PmValueError, "invalid hotkey combo '" & text & "'")
    if i < parts.high:
      case token
      of "ctrl": mods = mods or hmCtrl
      of "alt": mods = mods or hmAlt
      of "shift": mods = mods or hmShift
      of "super": mods = mods or hmSuper
      else:
        raise newException(
          PmValueError,
          "unknown hotkey modifier '" & part.strip() & "'; use ctrl, alt, shift and super"
        )
    elif isModifier(token):
      raise newException(
        PmValueError,
        "hotkey combo must end with a key; use mod+key, for example ctrl+alt+h"
      )
    else:
      let code = keyCode(token)
      return (comboString(mods, code), code, mods)
  raise newException(
    PmValueError,
    "hotkey combo must end with a key; use mod+key, for example ctrl+alt+h"
  )

proc isCallable(value: PyObject): bool =
  callObject(getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "callable"), value).to(bool)

proc osErrorSuffix(code: int): string =
  if code == 0:
    return ""
  when defined(windows):
    ": Windows error " & $code
  else:
    ": OS error " & $code

proc registrationError(combo: string, status: HotkeyStatus, code: int) =
  case status
  of hsOk:
    discard
  of hsConflict:
    raise newException(
      PmHotkeyError,
      "hotkey '" & combo & "' is already registered by another application"
    )
  of hsUnsupported:
    raise newException(
      PmHotkeyError,
      "hotkey '" & combo & "' cannot be registered on this system"
    )
  of hsFailure:
    raise newException(
      PmHotkeyError,
      "cannot register hotkey '" & combo & "'" & osErrorSuffix(code)
    )

proc unregistrationError(combo: string, code: int) =
  raise newException(
    PmHotkeyError,
    "cannot unregister hotkey '" & combo & "'" & osErrorSuffix(code)
  )

proc pump*() =
  if pumping:
    return
  pumping = true
  try:
    while true:
      let id = popEvent()
      if id < 0:
        break
      if not idIndex.hasKey(id):
        continue
      let index = idIndex[id]
      discard callObject(bindings[index].callback)
  finally:
    pumping = false

proc hotkeyBind*(
  value: PyObject,
  callback: PyObject = nil
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_hotkeyBind".} =
  pyWrap:
    let parsed = parseCombo(comboArg(value))
    if callback.isNil:
      raise newException(PmBadType, "hotkey callback is required")
    if not isCallable(callback):
      raise newException(PmBadType, "hotkey callback must be callable")
    if comboIndex.hasKey(parsed.combo):
      bindings[comboIndex[parsed.combo]].callback = callback
    else:
      if nextId > maxHotkeyId:
        raise newException(
          PmError,
          "too many hotkeys were bound in this process; restart to reset hotkey ids"
        )
      var errorCode = 0
      let status = hkAdd(nextId, parsed.code, parsed.mods, errorCode)
      registrationError(parsed.combo, status, errorCode)
      bindings.add HotkeyBinding(
        id: nextId,
        combo: parsed.combo,
        code: parsed.code,
        mods: parsed.mods,
        callback: callback
      )
      comboIndex[parsed.combo] = bindings.high
      idIndex[nextId] = bindings.high
      inc nextId

proc hotkeyUnbind*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_hotkeyUnbind".} =
  pyWrap:
    let parsed = parseCombo(comboArg(value))
    if comboIndex.hasKey(parsed.combo):
      let
        index = comboIndex[parsed.combo]
        id = bindings[index].id
      var errorCode = 0
      let status = hkRemove(id, errorCode)
      if status != hsOk:
        unregistrationError(parsed.combo, errorCode)
      idIndex.del(id)
      comboIndex.del(parsed.combo)
      bindings.delete(index)
      for i in index ..< bindings.len:
        comboIndex[bindings[i].combo] = i
        idIndex[bindings[i].id] = i

proc hotkeyList*(): seq[string] {.exportpy: "_hotkeyList".} =
  for binding in bindings:
    result.add binding.combo

proc hotkeyPoll*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_hotkeyPoll".} =
  pyWrap:
    pump()

```

`src/imgui_bridge.nim`:

```nim
import std/[sets, tables]
import nimpy
import raylib
from rlgl import GlVersion, drawRenderBatchActive, getVersion

import errors, keys, pyutil
import platform/api

type
  ImGuiBridge* = ref object
    module: PyObject
    io: PyObject
    backend: PyObject
    ownsContext: bool
    keyMap: Table[int, PyObject]
    downKeys: Table[int, bool]
    downMouse: Table[int, bool]
    frameOpen: bool

const imguiMouseButtons = [mbLeft, mbRight, mbMiddle, mbX1, mbX2]

proc imguiKeyName(code: int): string =
  case code
  of 32: "space"
  of 39: "apostrophe"
  of 44: "comma"
  of 45: "minus"
  of 46: "period"
  of 47: "slash"
  of 48..57: "_" & $char(code)
  of 59: "semicolon"
  of 61: "equal"
  of 65..90: $char(code + 32)
  of 91: "left_bracket"
  of 92: "backslash"
  of 93: "right_bracket"
  of 96: "grave_accent"
  of 256: "escape"
  of 257: "enter"
  of 258: "tab"
  of 259: "backspace"
  of 260: "insert"
  of 261: "delete"
  of 262: "right_arrow"
  of 263: "left_arrow"
  of 264: "down_arrow"
  of 265: "up_arrow"
  of 266: "page_up"
  of 267: "page_down"
  of 268: "home"
  of 269: "end"
  of 280: "caps_lock"
  of 281: "scroll_lock"
  of 282: "num_lock"
  of 283: "print_screen"
  of 284: "pause"
  of 290..301: "f" & $(code - 289)
  of 320..329: "keypad" & $(code - 320)
  of 330: "keypad_decimal"
  of 331: "keypad_divide"
  of 332: "keypad_multiply"
  of 333: "keypad_subtract"
  of 334: "keypad_add"
  of 335: "keypad_enter"
  of 336: "keypad_equal"
  of 340: "left_shift"
  of 341: "left_ctrl"
  of 342: "left_alt"
  of 343: "left_super"
  of 344: "right_shift"
  of 345: "right_ctrl"
  of 346: "right_alt"
  of 347: "right_super"
  of 348: "menu"
  else: ""

proc glslVersion(): string =
  case getVersion()
  of Opengl11Software, Opengl11: "#version 110"
  of Opengl21: "#version 120"
  of Opengl33, Opengl43: "#version 330"
  of OpenglEs20: "#version 100"
  of OpenglEs30: "#version 300 es"

proc loadModule(): PyObject =
  try:
    pyImport("imgui_bundle.imgui")
  except CatchableError as e:
    raise newException(
      PmModuleNotFound,
      "imgui_bundle is not available (" & e.msg &
        "); install it with 'pip install imgui-bundle'"
    )

proc buildKeyMap(module: PyObject): Table[int, PyObject] =
  let keyEnum = getAttr(module, "Key")
  for (_, code) in keyTable:
    let name = imguiKeyName(code)
    if name.len > 0:
      result[code] = getAttr(keyEnum, name.cstring)

proc openImGuiBundle*(): ImGuiBridge =
  let
    module = loadModule()
    keyMap = buildKeyMap(module)
  let ownsContext = isNone(callMethod(module, "get_current_context"))
  if ownsContext:
    discard callMethod(module, "create_context")
  let bridge = ImGuiBridge(
    module: module,
    ownsContext: ownsContext,
    keyMap: keyMap
  )
  try:
    bridge.io = callMethod(module, "get_io")
    if ownsContext:
      discard callMethod(bridge.io, "set_ini_filename", newPyNone())
    bridge.backend = getAttr(module, "backends")
    if not callObject(getAttr(bridge.backend, "opengl3_init"), glslVersion()).to(bool):
      raise newException(
        PmOverlayError,
        "the imgui_bundle OpenGL3 backend could not be initialized"
      )
  except CatchableError:
    if ownsContext:
      discard callMethod(module, "destroy_context")
    raise
  bridge

proc feedMouse(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  let
    position = mousePosition()
    origin = getWindowPosition()
  discard callMethod(
    bridge.io,
    "add_mouse_pos_event",
    float(position.x) - float(origin.x),
    float(position.y) - float(origin.y)
  )
  for index, button in imguiMouseButtons:
    let down = mouseButtonDown(button)
    if down != bridge.downMouse.getOrDefault(index, false):
      bridge.downMouse[index] = down
      discard callMethod(bridge.io, "add_mouse_button_event", index, down)

proc characterFor(code: int, shift: bool): string =
  case code
  of 32: " "
  of 48..57: $char(code)
  of 65..90: $char(if shift: code else: code + 32)
  else: ""

proc feedKeys(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  let held = keysDown().toHashSet()
  let shift = 340 in held or 344 in held
  for code, key in bridge.keyMap:
    let down = code in held
    if down != bridge.downKeys.getOrDefault(code, false):
      bridge.downKeys[code] = down
      discard callMethod(bridge.io, "add_key_event", key, down)
      if down:
        let text = characterFor(code, shift)
        if text.len > 0:
          discard callMethod(bridge.io, "add_input_characters_utf8", text)

proc feedInput(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  let size = callMethod(
    bridge.module,
    "ImVec2",
    float(getScreenWidth()),
    float(getScreenHeight())
  )
  setPyAttr(bridge.io, "display_size", nimValueToPy(size))
  setPyAttr(bridge.io, "delta_time", nimValueToPy(float(getFrameTime())))
  bridge.feedMouse()
  bridge.feedKeys()

proc setMouseDrawCursor*(bridge: ImGuiBridge, enabled: bool) =
  setPyAttr(bridge.io, "mouse_draw_cursor", nimValueToPy(enabled))

proc releaseInput*(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  for code, down in bridge.downKeys:
    if down:
      discard callMethod(bridge.io, "add_key_event", bridge.keyMap[code], false)
  bridge.downKeys.clear()
  for index, down in bridge.downMouse:
    if down:
      discard callMethod(bridge.io, "add_mouse_button_event", index, false)
  bridge.downMouse.clear()

proc beginImGui*(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  bridge.feedInput()
  discard callMethod(bridge.backend, "opengl3_new_frame")
  discard callMethod(bridge.module, "new_frame")
  bridge.frameOpen = true

proc endImGui*(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  if not bridge.frameOpen:
    return
  bridge.frameOpen = false
  discard callMethod(bridge.module, "render")
  let drawData = callMethod(bridge.module, "get_draw_data")
  drawRenderBatchActive()
  discard callMethod(bridge.backend, "opengl3_render_draw_data", drawData)

proc closeImGui*(bridge: ImGuiBridge) =
  try:
    discard callMethod(bridge.backend, "opengl3_shutdown")
    if bridge.ownsContext:
      discard callMethod(bridge.module, "destroy_context")
  except CatchableError:
    discard
```

`src/input.nim`:

```nim
import std/[math, tables]
import nimpy
import nimpy/py_types

import pyutil, errors, keys, vec
import platform/api

pyExportModule("pymew")

var
  keyStates: Table[int, bool]
  mouseStates: Table[MouseButton, bool]

const mouseButtons = [
  ("left", mbLeft),
  ("right", mbRight),
  ("middle", mbMiddle),
  ("x1", mbX1),
  ("x2", mbX2)
]

proc mouseButton(value: PyObject): MouseButton =
  if pyIsInstance(value, "bool"):
    raise newException(PmBadType, "mouse button must be a name or a number, not bool")
  if pyIsInstance(value, "int"):
    let number = value.to(int)
    if number < 1 or number > 255:
      raise newException(PmValueError, "mouse button number must be between 1 and 255")
    return MouseButton(number)
  if pyIsInstance(value, "str"):
    let
      text = value.to(string)
      name = normalizeName(text)
    for (candidate, button) in mouseButtons:
      if candidate == name:
        return button
    raise newException(
      PmValueError,
      "unknown mouse button '" & text & "'; use left, right, middle, x1, x2 or a number"
    )
  raise newException(PmBadType, "mouse button must be a str (name) or an int (number)")

proc edgePressed[T](states: var Table[T, bool], key: T, down: bool): bool =
  result = down and not states.getOrDefault(key, false)
  states[key] = down

proc edgeReleased[T](states: var Table[T, bool], key: T, down: bool): bool =
  result = not down and states.getOrDefault(key, false)
  states[key] = down

proc keyPressedState(code: int): bool =
  edgePressed(keyStates, code, keyDown(code))

proc keyReleasedState(code: int): bool =
  edgeReleased(keyStates, code, keyDown(code))

proc mousePressedState(button: MouseButton): bool =
  edgePressed(mouseStates, button, mouseButtonDown(button))

proc mouseReleasedState(button: MouseButton): bool =
  edgeReleased(mouseStates, button, mouseButtonDown(button))

proc inputKeyDown*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputKeyDown".} =
  pyWrap:
    keyDown(keyCodeValue(value))

proc inputKeyPressed*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputKeyPressed".} =
  pyWrap:
    keyPressedState(keyCodeValue(value))

proc inputKeyUp*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputKeyUp".} =
  pyWrap:
    keyReleasedState(keyCodeValue(value))

proc inputMousePos*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMousePos".} =
  pyWrap:
    let pos = mousePosition()
    newVec2(float(pos.x), float(pos.y))

proc inputMouseDown*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMouseDown".} =
  pyWrap:
    mouseButtonDown(mouseButton(value))

proc inputMousePressed*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMousePressed".} =
  pyWrap:
    mousePressedState(mouseButton(value))

proc inputMouseUp*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMouseUp".} =
  pyWrap:
    mouseReleasedState(mouseButton(value))

proc inputMouseButtons*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMouseButtons".} =
  pyWrap:
    var items: seq[PPyObject]
    for number in 1 .. mouseButtonCount():
      items.add nimValueToPy(number)
    newPyListTaking(items)

proc inputKeyPress*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputKeyPress".} =
  pyWrap:
    let code = keyCodeValue(value)
    injectKey(code, true)
    injectKey(code, false)

proc inputMouseMove*(
  x, y: float,
  relative: bool = false
): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMouseMove".} =
  pyWrap:
    injectMouseMove(int(round(x)), int(round(y)), relative)

proc inputMouseClick*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMouseClick".} =
  pyWrap:
    let button = mouseButton(value)
    injectMouseButton(button, true)
    injectMouseButton(button, false)

proc inputMouseScroll*(amount: float): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputMouseScroll".} =
  pyWrap:
    injectMouseScroll(amount)

proc inputSetPos*(x, y: float): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_inputSetPos".} =
  pyWrap:
    setCursorPos(int(round(x)), int(round(y)))

```

`src/keys.nim`:

```nim
import nimpy
import nimpy/py_types

import pyutil, errors

pyExportModule("pymew")

const keyTable* = [
  ("a", 65), ("b", 66), ("c", 67), ("d", 68), ("e", 69), ("f", 70), ("g", 71),
  ("h", 72), ("i", 73), ("j", 74), ("k", 75), ("l", 76), ("m", 77), ("n", 78),
  ("o", 79), ("p", 80), ("q", 81), ("r", 82), ("s", 83), ("t", 84), ("u", 85),
  ("v", 86), ("w", 87), ("x", 88), ("y", 89), ("z", 90),
  ("0", 48), ("1", 49), ("2", 50), ("3", 51), ("4", 52), ("5", 53), ("6", 54),
  ("7", 55), ("8", 56), ("9", 57),
  ("apostrophe", 39), ("comma", 44), ("minus", 45), ("period", 46), ("slash", 47),
  ("semicolon", 59), ("equal", 61), ("leftbracket", 91), ("backslash", 92),
  ("rightbracket", 93), ("grave", 96),
  ("space", 32),
  ("escape", 256), ("enter", 257), ("tab", 258), ("backspace", 259),
  ("insert", 260), ("delete", 261), ("right", 262), ("left", 263), ("down", 264),
  ("up", 265), ("pageup", 266), ("pagedown", 267), ("home", 268), ("end", 269),
  ("capslock", 280), ("scrolllock", 281), ("numlock", 282),
  ("printscreen", 283), ("pause", 284),
  ("f1", 290), ("f2", 291), ("f3", 292), ("f4", 293), ("f5", 294), ("f6", 295),
  ("f7", 296), ("f8", 297), ("f9", 298), ("f10", 299), ("f11", 300), ("f12", 301),
  ("lshift", 340), ("lctrl", 341), ("lalt", 342), ("lsuper", 343),
  ("rshift", 344), ("rctrl", 345), ("ralt", 346), ("rsuper", 347), ("menu", 348),
  ("num0", 320), ("num1", 321), ("num2", 322), ("num3", 323), ("num4", 324),
  ("num5", 325), ("num6", 326), ("num7", 327), ("num8", 328), ("num9", 329),
  ("numdecimal", 330), ("numdivide", 331), ("nummultiply", 332),
  ("numsubtract", 333), ("numadd", 334), ("numenter", 335), ("numequal", 336)
]

proc findKeyName(name: string): int =
  let want = normalizeName(name)
  for i, (candidate, _) in keyTable:
    if candidate == want:
      return i
  -1

proc findKeyCode(code: int): int =
  for i, (_, candidate) in keyTable:
    if candidate == code:
      return i
  -1

proc suggestKey(name: string): string =
  var candidates: seq[string]
  for (candidate, _) in keyTable:
    candidates.add candidate
  closestName(normalizeName(name), candidates, 2)

proc keyCode*(name: string): int =
  let i = findKeyName(name)
  if i >= 0:
    return keyTable[i][1]
  var msg = "unknown key '" & name & "'; see pm.keys.names() for valid names"
  let suggestion = suggestKey(name)
  if suggestion.len > 0:
    msg.add "; did you mean '" & suggestion & "'?"
  raise newException(PmValueError, msg)

proc keyName*(code: int): string =
  let i = findKeyCode(code)
  if i >= 0:
    return keyTable[i][0]
  raise newException(PmValueError, "unknown key code " & $code & "; see pm.keys.names() for valid names")

proc keyNames*(): seq[string] =
  for (name, _) in keyTable:
    result.add name

proc keyCodeValue*(value: PyObject): int =
  if pyIsInstance(value, "bool"):
    raise newException(PmBadType, "key must be a name or a code, not bool")
  if pyIsInstance(value, "int"):
    let code = value.to(int)
    if findKeyCode(code) < 0:
      raise newException(
        PmValueError,
        "unknown key code " & $code & "; see pm.keys.names() for valid names"
      )
    return code
  if pyIsInstance(value, "str"):
    return keyCode(value.to(string))
  raise newException(PmBadType, "key must be a str (name) or an int (code)")

proc keyCodeOs*(code: int): int =
  when defined(windows):
    case code
    of 32: 0x20
    of 39: 0xDE
    of 44: 0xBC
    of 45: 0xBD
    of 46: 0xBE
    of 47: 0xBF
    of 48..57: code
    of 59: 0xBA
    of 61: 0xBB
    of 65..90: code
    of 91: 0xDB
    of 92: 0xDC
    of 93: 0xDD
    of 96: 0xC0
    of 256: 0x1B
    of 257: 0x0D
    of 258: 0x09
    of 259: 0x08
    of 260: 0x2D
    of 261: 0x2E
    of 262: 0x27
    of 263: 0x25
    of 264: 0x28
    of 265: 0x26
    of 266: 0x21
    of 267: 0x22
    of 268: 0x24
    of 269: 0x23
    of 280: 0x14
    of 281: 0x91
    of 282: 0x90
    of 283: 0x2C
    of 284: 0x13
    of 290..301: 0x70 + (code - 290)
    of 320..329: 0x60 + (code - 320)
    of 330: 0x6E
    of 331: 0x6F
    of 332: 0x6A
    of 333: 0x6D
    of 334: 0x6B
    of 335: 0x0D
    of 336: 0x92
    of 340: 0xA0
    of 341: 0xA2
    of 342: 0xA4
    of 343: 0x5B
    of 344: 0xA1
    of 345: 0xA3
    of 346: 0xA5
    of 347: 0x5C
    of 348: 0x5D
    else: 0
  else:
    case code
    of 32: 0x20
    of 39: 0x27
    of 44: 0x2C
    of 45: 0x2D
    of 46: 0x2E
    of 47: 0x2F
    of 48..57: code
    of 59: 0x3B
    of 61: 0x3D
    of 65..90: code + 32
    of 91: 0x5B
    of 92: 0x5C
    of 93: 0x5D
    of 96: 0x60
    of 256: 0xFF1B
    of 257: 0xFF0D
    of 258: 0xFF09
    of 259: 0xFF08
    of 260: 0xFF63
    of 261: 0xFFFF
    of 262: 0xFF53
    of 263: 0xFF51
    of 264: 0xFF54
    of 265: 0xFF52
    of 266: 0xFF55
    of 267: 0xFF56
    of 268: 0xFF50
    of 269: 0xFF57
    of 280: 0xFFE5
    of 281: 0xFF14
    of 282: 0xFF7F
    of 283: 0xFF61
    of 284: 0xFF13
    of 290..301: 0xFFBE + (code - 290)
    of 320..329: 0xFFB0 + (code - 320)
    of 330: 0xFFAE
    of 331: 0xFFAF
    of 332: 0xFFAA
    of 333: 0xFFAD
    of 334: 0xFFAB
    of 335: 0xFF8D
    of 336: 0xFFBD
    of 340: 0xFFE1
    of 341: 0xFFE3
    of 342: 0xFFE9
    of 343: 0xFFEB
    of 344: 0xFFE2
    of 345: 0xFFE4
    of 346: 0xFFEA
    of 347: 0xFFEC
    of 348: 0xFF67
    else: 0

proc keysCode*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_keyCode".} =
  pyWrap:
    keyCodeValue(value)

proc keysName*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_keyName".} =
  pyWrap:
    if pyIsInstance(value, "bool") or not pyIsInstance(value, "int"):
      raise newException(PmBadType, "key code must be an int")
    keyName(value.to(int))

proc keysNames*(): seq[string] {.exportpy: "_keyNames".} =
  keyNames()
```

`src/mem.nim`:

```nim
import std/[strutils, sequtils, algorithm, os, dynlib, times]
from std/unicode import runes
import nimpy
import nimpy/[py_types, py_utils, py_lib as pyl]

import pyutil, errors, mem_types, vec
import platform/api

pyExportModule("pymew")

type
  Process* = ref object of PyNimObjectExperimental
    pid*: int
    debugFlag*: bool
    handle*: OsHandle
    closed*: bool
    is64Known*: bool
    is64Cache*: bool

proc `$`*(self: Process): string =
  "Process(pid=" & $self.pid & ")"

proc sizeOfDesc*(o: PyObject): int
```
