`src/platform/common.nim`:

```nim
import std/locks

type
  OsHandle* = int

  RawProcess* = object
    pid*: int
    name*: string

  RawModule* = object
    name*: string
    path*: string
    base*: uint
    `end`*: uint
    size*: uint

  RawRegion* = object
    start*: uint
    `end`*: uint
    size*: uint
    protection*: string

  RawWindow* = object
    id*: int
    pid*: int
    name*: string
    title*: string
    x*: int
    y*: int
    width*: int
    height*: int
    visible*: bool

  RawMonitor* = object
    name*: string
    x*: int
    y*: int
    width*: int
    height*: int
    refresh*: int

  OsFailure* = object of CatchableError
    code*: int
    denied*: bool

  OsUnsupported* = object of CatchableError

  MouseButton* = range[1..255]

  HotkeyStatus* = enum
    hsOk
    hsConflict
    hsFailure
    hsUnsupported

  HotkeyOpKind* = enum
    hoAdd
    hoRemove
    hoStop

  HotkeyOp* = object
    kind*: HotkeyOpKind
    id*: int
    code*: int
    mods*: uint8
    status*: HotkeyStatus
    errorCode*: int
    done*: bool

  WakeProc* = proc() {.nimcall.}

const
  mbLeft* = MouseButton(1)
  mbRight* = MouseButton(2)
  mbMiddle* = MouseButton(3)
  mbX1* = MouseButton(4)
  mbX2* = MouseButton(5)

  hkMaxEvents* = 1024

  hmCtrl* = 1'u8
  hmAlt* = 2'u8
  hmShift* = 4'u8
  hmSuper* = 8'u8

var
  stateInitialized: bool
  hkLock: Lock
  hkCond: Cond
  workerReady: bool
  workerStartStatus: HotkeyStatus
  currentOp: HotkeyOp
  opQueued: bool
  opTaken: bool
  events: array[hkMaxEvents, int]
  eventHead: int
  eventTail: int
  eventCount: int

proc initHotkeyState*() =
  if stateInitialized:
    return
  initLock(hkLock)
  initCond(hkCond)
  stateInitialized = true

proc markWorkerReady*(status: HotkeyStatus) =
  withLock hkLock:
    workerStartStatus = status
    workerReady = true
    broadcast(hkCond)

proc resetWorkerState*() =
  withLock hkLock:
    workerReady = false
    workerStartStatus = hsOk

proc waitWorkerReady*(): HotkeyStatus =
  withLock hkLock:
    while not workerReady:
      wait(hkCond, hkLock)
    result = workerStartStatus

proc submitOp*(op: HotkeyOp, wake: WakeProc, errorCode: var int): HotkeyStatus =
  withLock hkLock:
    while opQueued:
      wait(hkCond, hkLock)
    currentOp = op
    currentOp.done = false
    opQueued = true
    opTaken = false
  wake()
  withLock hkLock:
    while not currentOp.done:
      wait(hkCond, hkLock)
    result = currentOp.status
    errorCode = currentOp.errorCode
    opQueued = false

proc takeOp*(op: var HotkeyOp): bool =
  withLock hkLock:
    if opQueued and not opTaken:
      opTaken = true
      op = currentOp
      result = true

proc finishOp*(status: HotkeyStatus, errorCode: int = 0) =
  withLock hkLock:
    currentOp.status = status
    currentOp.errorCode = errorCode
    currentOp.done = true
    signal(hkCond)

proc pushEvent*(id: int) =
  withLock hkLock:
    if eventCount < hkMaxEvents:
      events[eventTail] = id
      eventTail = (eventTail + 1) mod hkMaxEvents
      inc eventCount

proc popEvent*(): int =
  withLock hkLock:
    if eventCount > 0:
      result = events[eventHead]
      eventHead = (eventHead + 1) mod hkMaxEvents
      dec eventCount
    else:
      result = -1

proc betterWindow*(a, b: RawWindow): bool =
  if a.visible != b.visible:
    return a.visible
  a.width * a.height > b.width * b.height

proc pickWindow*(windows: seq[RawWindow], pid: int): int =
  result = -1
  for i, window in windows:
    if window.pid != pid:
      continue
    if result < 0 or betterWindow(window, windows[result]):
      result = i
```

`src/platform/linux.nim`:

```nim
import std/[os, strutils, tables, algorithm]
import std/posix
import x11/[x, xlib, xatom, xrandr]

import common
from ./x11_input import openDisplay, rootWindow

proc process_vm_readv(
  pid: Pid,
  localIov: ptr IOVec,
  liovcnt: culong,
  remoteIov: ptr IOVec,
  riovcnt: culong,
  flags: culong
): int {.importc, header: "<sys/uio.h>".}

proc process_vm_writev(
  pid: Pid,
  localIov: ptr IOVec,
  liovcnt: culong,
  remoteIov: ptr IOVec,
  riovcnt: culong,
  flags: culong
): int {.importc, header: "<sys/uio.h>".}

proc osFailure(op: string, code: OSErrorCode): ref OsFailure =
  result = newException(OsFailure, op & " failed: " & osErrorMsg(code))
  result.code = code.int
  let c = code.int32
  result.denied = c == EPERM.int32 or c == EACCES.int32

proc permissionHint*(): string =
  "permission denied: Linux allows process_vm reads only for child processes " &
  "unless the tracer has CAP_SYS_PTRACE; set kernel.yama.ptrace_scope=0 " &
  "(sudo sysctl kernel.yama.ptrace_scope=0) or run with sudo/root"

proc processExists*(pid: int): bool

proc openProcessHandle*(pid: int): OsHandle =
  if pid <= 0 or not processExists(pid):
    raise osFailure("open process " & $pid, OSErrorCode(ESRCH))
  result = pid

proc closeProcessHandle*(h: OsHandle) =
  discard

proc readMemory*(h: OsHandle, address: uint, dst: pointer, size: int): int =
  if size == 0:
    return 0
  var local, remote: IOVec
  local.iov_base = dst
  local.iov_len = size.uint
  remote.iov_base = cast[pointer](address)
  remote.iov_len = size.uint
  let n = process_vm_readv(Pid(h), addr local, 1, addr remote, 1, 0)
  if n < 0:
    raise osFailure("read", osLastError())
  result = n

proc writeMemory*(h: OsHandle, address: uint, src: pointer, size: int): int =
  if size == 0:
    return 0
  var local, remote: IOVec
  local.iov_base = src
  local.iov_len = size.uint
  remote.iov_base = cast[pointer](address)
  remote.iov_len = size.uint
  let n = process_vm_writev(Pid(h), addr local, 1, addr remote, 1, 0)
  if n < 0:
    raise osFailure("write", osLastError())
  result = n

proc readComm(pid: int): string =
  try:
    result = readFile("/proc/" & $pid & "/comm").strip()
  except CatchableError:
    discard

proc statState(pid: int): char =
  let
    stat = readFile("/proc/" & $pid & "/stat")
    closeParen = stat.rfind(')')
  if closeParen < 0 or closeParen + 2 >= stat.len:
    return '\0'
  result = stat[closeParen + 2]

proc processExists*(pid: int): bool =
  if pid <= 0:
    return false
  try:
    let state = statState(pid)
    result = state notin {'\0', 'Z', 'X', 'x'}
  except CatchableError:
    result = false

proc readCmdlineName(pid: int): string =
  try:
    let
      raw = readFile("/proc/" & $pid & "/cmdline")
      nul = raw.find('\0')
      argv0 = if nul >= 0: raw[0 ..< nul] else: raw
    if argv0.len > 0:
      result = extractFilename(argv0)
  except CatchableError:
    discard

proc enumProcesses*(): seq[RawProcess] =
  for kind, path in walkDir("/proc", relative = true):
    if kind != pcDir and kind != pcLinkToDir:
      continue
    if path.len == 0 or path[0] notin {'0'..'9'}:
      continue
    var pid: int
    try:
      pid = parseInt(path)
    except ValueError:
      continue
    try:
      result.add RawProcess(pid: pid, name: readComm(pid))
    except CatchableError:
      discard

proc processNameByPid*(pid: int): string =
  readComm(pid)

proc processPath*(pid: int): string =
  var buf = newString(4096)
  let n = readlink(("/proc/" & $pid & "/exe").cstring, buf.cstring, buf.len)
  if n <= 0:
    raise osFailure("readlink /proc/" & $pid & "/exe", osLastError())
  result = buf[0 ..< n]

proc readExeName(pid: int): string =
  try:
    result = extractFilename(processPath(pid))
  except CatchableError:
    discard

proc processIs64Bit*(pid: int): bool =
  var f: File
  if not open(f, "/proc/" & $pid & "/exe", fmRead):
    raise osFailure("open /proc/" & $pid & "/exe", osLastError())
  defer: f.close()
  var header: array[5, byte]
  if f.readBuffer(addr header[0], header.len) != header.len:
    raise osFailure("read ELF header of " & $pid, OSErrorCode(5))
  if header[0] != 0x7F'u8 or header[1] != 'E'.ord.uint8 or header[2] != 'L'.ord.uint8 or header[3] != 'F'.ord.uint8:
    raise osFailure("validate ELF header of " & $pid, OSErrorCode(5))
  result = header[4] == 2

proc parseMapLine(line: string): tuple[start, stop: uint, perms, path: string] =
  let fields = line.splitWhitespace(maxsplit = 5)
  if fields.len < 5:
    return
  let dash = fields[0].find('-')
  if dash < 0:
    return
  try:
    result.start = uint(parseHexInt(fields[0][0 ..< dash]))
    result.stop = uint(parseHexInt(fields[0][dash + 1 .. ^1]))
  except ValueError:
    return
  result.perms = fields[1]
  if fields.len > 5:
    result.path = fields[5]

proc stripDeleted(path: string): string =
  result = path
  if result.endsWith(" (deleted)"):
    result.setLen(result.len - 10)

proc isModulePath(path: string): bool =
  if path.len == 0 or path[0] != '/':
    return false
  not (path.startsWith("/dev/") or path.startsWith("/memfd:") or path.startsWith("/SYSV"))

proc protectionOf(perms: string): string =
  if perms.len < 4:
    return perms
  result = perms[0 .. 2]
  if perms[3] == 'p':
    result.add 'p'

type ModuleAgg = object
  name, path: string
  base, stop: uint

iterator procMaps(pid: int): string =
  let path = "/proc/" & $pid & "/maps"
  var f: File
  if not open(f, path, fmRead):
    raise osFailure("open " & path, osLastError())
  defer: f.close()
  for line in f.lines:
    yield line

proc enumModules*(pid: int): seq[RawModule] =
  var byPath: Table[string, ModuleAgg]
  var order: seq[string]
  for line in procMaps(pid):
    let entry = parseMapLine(line)
    if not isModulePath(entry.path):
      continue
    let cleanPath = stripDeleted(entry.path)
    if not byPath.hasKey(cleanPath):
      byPath[cleanPath] = ModuleAgg(
        name: extractFilename(cleanPath),
        path: cleanPath,
        base: entry.start,
        stop: entry.stop
      )
      order.add cleanPath
    else:
      byPath[cleanPath].base = min(byPath[cleanPath].base, entry.start)
      byPath[cleanPath].stop = max(byPath[cleanPath].stop, entry.stop)
  var modules: seq[RawModule]
  for p in order:
    let agg = byPath[p]
    modules.add RawModule(name: agg.name, path: agg.path, base: agg.base, `end`: agg.stop, size: agg.stop - agg.base)
  modules.sort(proc(a, b: RawModule): int = cmp(a.base, b.base))
  result = modules

proc enumRegions*(pid: int): seq[RawRegion] =
  for line in procMaps(pid):
    let entry = parseMapLine(line)
    if entry.stop <= entry.start:
      continue
    result.add RawRegion(
      start: entry.start,
      `end`: entry.stop,
      size: entry.stop - entry.start,
      protection: protectionOf(entry.perms)
    )

proc processNames*(pid: int): seq[string] =
  var seen: seq[string]
  for n in [readComm(pid), readExeName(pid), readCmdlineName(pid)]:
    if n.len > 0:
      let lower = n.toLowerAscii()
      if lower notin seen:
        seen.add lower
  result = seen

proc systemFontDirs*(): seq[string] =
  let
    home = getHomeDir()
    dataHome = getEnv("XDG_DATA_HOME")
    dataDirsEnv = getEnv("XDG_DATA_DIRS")
  result.add home / ".fonts"
  result.add home / ".local" / "share" / "fonts"
  if dataHome.len > 0:
    result.add dataHome / "fonts"
  let dataDirs =
    if dataDirsEnv.len > 0: dataDirsEnv.split(':')
    else: @["/usr/local/share", "/usr/share"]
  for dir in dataDirs:
    if dir.len > 0:
      result.add dir / "fonts"
  var seen: seq[string]
  var unique: seq[string]
  for dir in result:
    let key = dir.toLowerAscii()
    if key notin seen:
      seen.add key
      unique.add dir
  result = unique

type
  XRRCrtcInfo {.final.} = object
    timestamp*: x.Time
    x*, y*: cint
    width*, height*: cuint
    mode*: RRMode
    rotation*: Rotation
    noutput*: cint
    outputs*: ptr UncheckedArray[RROutput]
    rotations*: Rotation
    npossible*: cint
    possible*: ptr UncheckedArray[RROutput]

proc XRRGetCrtcInfo(
  dpy: PDisplay,
  res: PXRRScreenResources,
  crtc: RRCrtc
): ptr XRRCrtcInfo {.cdecl, dynlib: libXrandr, importc.}

proc XRRFreeCrtcInfo(info: ptr XRRCrtcInfo) {.cdecl, dynlib: libXrandr, importc.}

proc x11ErrorHandler(d: PDisplay, event: PXErrorEvent): cint {.cdecl.} =
  discard d
  discard event
  0

proc prepareWindowQueries() =
  discard XSetErrorHandler(x11ErrorHandler)

proc internAtom(d: PDisplay, name: string): Atom =
  XInternAtom(d, name.cstring, 0)

proc readProperty(
  d: PDisplay,
  win: Window,
  name: string,
  reqType: Atom
): tuple[format: cint, data: seq[byte]] =
  let atom = internAtom(d, name)
  if atom == 0:
    return
  var
    actualType: Atom
    actualFormat: cint
    nitems, bytesAfter: culong
    raw: ptr uint8
  if XGetWindowProperty(
    d,
    win,
    atom,
    0,
    65536,
    0,
    reqType,
    addr actualType,
    addr actualFormat,
    addr nitems,
    addr bytesAfter,
    cast[PPcuchar](addr raw)
  ) != Success:
    return
  if raw.isNil:
    return
  defer: discard XFree(cast[pointer](raw))
  result.format = actualFormat
  let
    unit = if actualFormat == 32: sizeof(clong) else: 1
    total = int(nitems) * unit
  result.data = newSeq[byte](total)
  if total > 0:
    copyMem(addr result.data[0], raw, total)

proc propertyWindows(d: PDisplay, win: Window, name: string): seq[Window] =
  let prop = readProperty(d, win, name, XA_WINDOW)
  if prop.format != 32:
    return
  let count = prop.data.len div sizeof(clong)
  for i in 0 ..< count:
    var value: clong
    copyMem(addr value, unsafeAddr prop.data[i * sizeof(clong)], sizeof(clong))
    result.add Window(value)

proc propertyCardinal(d: PDisplay, win: Window, name: string): uint =
  let prop = readProperty(d, win, name, XA_CARDINAL)
  if prop.format != 32 or prop.data.len < sizeof(clong):
    return 0
  var value: clong
  copyMem(addr value, unsafeAddr prop.data[0], sizeof(clong))
  uint(value and 0xFFFFFFFF'i64)

proc propertyText(d: PDisplay, win: Window, name: string): string =
  let prop = readProperty(d, win, name, AnyPropertyType)
  if prop.format != 8 or prop.data.len == 0:
    return
  result = newString(prop.data.len)
  copyMem(addr result[0], unsafeAddr prop.data[0], prop.data.len)
  let nul = result.find('\0')
  if nul >= 0:
    result.setLen(nul)

proc windowPid(d: PDisplay, win: Window): int =
  let direct = propertyCardinal(d, win, "_NET_WM_PID")
  if direct > 0:
    return int(direct)
  let leaders = propertyWindows(d, win, "WM_CLIENT_LEADER")
  for leader in leaders:
    if leader == 0 or leader == win:
      continue
    let inherited = propertyCardinal(d, leader, "_NET_WM_PID")
    if inherited > 0:
      return int(inherited)
  0

proc windowProcessName(pid: int): string =
  try:
    processNameByPid(pid)
  except OsFailure:
    ""

proc addWindow(
  d: PDisplay,
  win: Window,
  seen: var seq[Window],
  items: var seq[RawWindow]
) =
  if win in seen:
    return
  seen.add win
  var attributes: XWindowAttributes
  if XGetWindowAttributes(d, win, addr attributes) == 0:
    return
  let pid = windowPid(d, win)
  if pid <= 0:
    return
  var rootX, rootY: cint
  var child: Window
  if XTranslateCoordinates(d, win, rootWindow, 0, 0, addr rootX, addr rootY, addr child) == 0:
    return
  var title = propertyText(d, win, "_NET_WM_NAME")
  if title.len == 0:
    title = propertyText(d, win, "WM_NAME")
  items.add RawWindow(
    id: int(win),
    pid: pid,
    name: windowProcessName(pid),
    title: title,
    x: rootX.int,
    y: rootY.int,
    width: attributes.width.int,
    height: attributes.height.int,
    visible: attributes.map_state == IsViewable
  )

proc walkWindowTree(
  d: PDisplay,
  win: Window,
  depth: int,
  seen: var seq[Window],
  items: var seq[RawWindow]
) =
  if depth > 16:
    return
  var
    root, parent: Window
    children: PWindow
    count: cuint
  if XQueryTree(d, win, addr root, addr parent, addr children, addr count) == 0:
    return
  defer:
    if not children.isNil:
      discard XFree(cast[pointer](children))
  for i in 0 ..< int(count):
    let child = cast[ptr UncheckedArray[Window]](children)[i]
    var attributes: XWindowAttributes
    if XGetWindowAttributes(d, child, addr attributes) == 0:
      continue
    if attributes.override_redirect != 0 or attributes.map_state != IsViewable:
      continue
    addWindow(d, child, seen, items)
    walkWindowTree(d, child, depth + 1, seen, items)

proc windowList*(): seq[RawWindow] =
  let d = openDisplay()
  prepareWindowQueries()
  var seen: seq[Window]
  for win in propertyWindows(d, rootWindow, "_NET_CLIENT_LIST"):
    addWindow(d, win, seen, result)
  if result.len == 0:
    walkWindowTree(d, rootWindow, 0, seen, result)

proc findWindow*(pid: int): RawWindow =
  let
    windows = windowList()
    index = pickWindow(windows, pid)
  if index < 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "no window found for process " & $pid)
  result = windows[index]

proc windowGeometry*(id: int): RawWindow =
  let d = openDisplay()
  prepareWindowQueries()
  var attributes: XWindowAttributes
  if XGetWindowAttributes(d, Window(id), addr attributes) == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "window " & $id & " no longer exists")
  var rootX, rootY: cint
  var child: Window
  if XTranslateCoordinates(
    d,
    Window(id),
    rootWindow,
    0,
    0,
    addr rootX,
    addr rootY,
    addr child
  ) == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "window " & $id & " no longer exists")
  RawWindow(
    id: id,
    x: rootX.int,
    y: rootY.int,
    width: attributes.width.int,
    height: attributes.height.int,
    visible: attributes.map_state == IsViewable
  )

proc screenSize*(): tuple[width, height: int] =
  let
    d = openDisplay()
    screen = XDefaultScreen(d)
  (XDisplayWidth(d, screen).int, XDisplayHeight(d, screen).int)

proc monitorList*(): seq[RawMonitor] =
  let
    d = openDisplay()
    screen = XDefaultScreen(d)
    resources = XRRGetScreenResourcesCurrent(d, rootWindow)
  if resources.isNil:
    return @[RawMonitor(
      name: "",
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      width: XDisplayWidth(d, screen).int,
      height: XDisplayHeight(d, screen).int,
      refresh: 0
    )]
  defer:
    XRRFreeScreenResources(resources)
  var seen: seq[RRCrtc]
  for i in 0 ..< resources.noutput.int:
    let
      output = resources.outputs[i]
      info = XRRGetOutputInfo(d, resources, output)
    if info.isNil:
      continue
    try:
      if info.connection != RR_Connected or info.crtc == 0:
        continue
      if info.crtc in seen:
        continue
      seen.add info.crtc
      let crtc = XRRGetCrtcInfo(d, resources, info.crtc)
      if crtc.isNil:
        continue
      try:
        if crtc.width <= 0 or crtc.height <= 0:
          continue
        var refresh = 0
        if crtc.mode != 0:
          for j in 0 ..< resources.nmode.int:
            let mode = resources.modes[j]
            if mode.id == crtc.mode:
              let pixels = int64(mode.htotal) * int64(mode.vtotal)
              if pixels > 0:
                refresh = int(float64(mode.dotClock) / float64(pixels) + 0.5)
              break
        result.add RawMonitor(
          name: if info.name.isNil: "" else: $info.name,
          x: crtc.x.int,
          y: crtc.y.int,
          width: crtc.width.int,
          height: crtc.height.int,
          refresh: refresh
        )
      finally:
        XRRFreeCrtcInfo(crtc)
    finally:
      XRRFreeOutputInfo(info)
  if result.len == 0:
    result.add RawMonitor(
      name: "",
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      width: XDisplayWidth(d, screen).int,
      height: XDisplayHeight(d, screen).int,
      refresh: 0
    )

```

`src/platform/win32_hotkey.nim`:

```nim
import std/typedthreads
import winim/lean

import common
import ../keys

var
  wakeEvent: HANDLE
  worker: Thread[int]
  started: bool

proc hotkeyFlags(mods: uint8): UINT =
  result = UINT(MOD_NOREPEAT)
  if (mods and hmCtrl) != 0:
    result = result or UINT(MOD_CONTROL)
  if (mods and hmAlt) != 0:
    result = result or UINT(MOD_ALT)
  if (mods and hmShift) != 0:
    result = result or UINT(MOD_SHIFT)
  if (mods and hmSuper) != 0:
    result = result or UINT(MOD_WIN)

proc registerHotkey(op: HotkeyOp): tuple[status: HotkeyStatus, errorCode: int] =
  let vk = keyCodeOs(op.code)
  if vk == 0:
    return (hsUnsupported, 0)
  if RegisterHotKey(0, int32(op.id), hotkeyFlags(op.mods), UINT(vk)) != 0:
    return (hsOk, 0)
  let code = GetLastError().int
  if code == ERROR_HOTKEY_ALREADY_REGISTERED:
    (hsConflict, code)
  else:
    (hsFailure, code)

proc unregisterHotkey(op: HotkeyOp): tuple[status: HotkeyStatus, errorCode: int] =
  if UnregisterHotKey(0, int32(op.id)) != 0:
    (hsOk, 0)
  else:
    (hsFailure, GetLastError().int)

proc processOps() =
  var op: HotkeyOp
  while takeOp(op):
    case op.kind
    of hoAdd:
      let outcome = registerHotkey(op)
      finishOp(outcome.status, outcome.errorCode)
    of hoRemove:
      let outcome = unregisterHotkey(op)
      finishOp(outcome.status, outcome.errorCode)
    of hoStop:
      finishOp(hsOk)

proc hotkeyWorker(_: int) {.thread.} =
  var msg: MSG
  discard PeekMessage(addr msg, 0, 0, 0, UINT(PM_NOREMOVE))
  markWorkerReady(hsOk)
  while true:
    let ready = MsgWaitForMultipleObjects(
      1,
      addr wakeEvent,
      FALSE,
      DWORD(INFINITE),
      DWORD(QS_ALLINPUT)
    )
    if ready == DWORD(WAIT_OBJECT_0):
      discard ResetEvent(wakeEvent)
    while PeekMessage(addr msg, 0, 0, 0, UINT(PM_REMOVE)) != 0:
      if msg.message == UINT(WM_HOTKEY):
        pushEvent(int(msg.wParam))
    processOps()

proc hkWake() =
  discard SetEvent(wakeEvent)

proc hkStart*() =
  if started:
    return
  initHotkeyState()
  resetWorkerState()
  wakeEvent = CreateEvent(nil, FALSE, FALSE, nil)
  if wakeEvent == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "cannot create the hotkey wake event")
  try:
    createThread(worker, hotkeyWorker, 0)
  except CatchableError:
    wakeEvent = 0
    raise
  started = true
  let status = waitWorkerReady()
  if status != hsOk:
    started = false
    raise newException(OsUnsupported, "cannot start the hotkey worker thread")

proc hkAdd*(id, code: int, mods: uint8, errorCode: var int): HotkeyStatus =
  hkStart()
  submitOp(HotkeyOp(kind: hoAdd, id: id, code: code, mods: mods), hkWake, errorCode)

proc hkRemove*(id: int, errorCode: var int): HotkeyStatus =
  if not started:
    return hsOk
  submitOp(HotkeyOp(kind: hoRemove, id: id), hkWake, errorCode)
```

`src/platform/win32_input.nim`:

```nim
import std/math
import winim/lean

import common
import ../keys

proc inputFailure(op: string): ref OsFailure =
  let code = GetLastError().int
  var msg = op & " failed: Windows error " & $code
  if code == ERROR_REQUIRES_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWSTATION:
    msg.add " (needs an interactive desktop session, not a service/SSH session)"
  newException(OsFailure, msg)

proc sendInput(inp: var INPUT, op: string) =
  if SendInput(1, addr inp, sizeof(INPUT).int32) == 0:
    raise inputFailure(op)

proc vkDown(vk: int32): bool =
  (int(GetAsyncKeyState(vk)) and 0x8000) != 0

proc keyDown*(code: int): bool =
  let vk = keyCodeOs(code)
  vk != 0 and vkDown(int32(vk))

proc keysDown*(): seq[int] =
  for (_, code) in keyTable:
    let vk = keyCodeOs(code)
    if vk != 0 and vkDown(int32(vk)):
      result.add code

proc mousePosition*(): tuple[x, y: int] =
  var p: POINT
  if GetCursorPos(addr p) == 0:
    raise inputFailure("read cursor position")
  (p.x.int, p.y.int)

proc mouseButtonVk(button: MouseButton): int32 =
  case button
  of mbLeft: VK_LBUTTON
  of mbRight: VK_RBUTTON
  of mbMiddle: VK_MBUTTON
  of mbX1: VK_XBUTTON1
  of mbX2: VK_XBUTTON2
  else:
    raise newException(
      OsUnsupported,
      "Windows exposes only left, right, middle, x1 and x2 through GetAsyncKeyState"
    )

proc mouseButtonDown*(button: MouseButton): bool =
  (int(GetAsyncKeyState(mouseButtonVk(button))) and 0x8000) != 0

proc mouseButtonCount*(): int =
  5

proc isExtended(code: int): bool =
  case code
  of 260, 261, 262, 263, 264, 265, 266, 267, 268, 269, 282, 283, 331, 335, 345, 346:
    true
  else:
    false

proc injectKey*(code: int, down: bool) =
  var inp: INPUT
  inp.`type` = INPUT_KEYBOARD
  inp.ki.wVk = WORD(keyCodeOs(code))
  if not down:
    inp.ki.dwFlags = DWORD(KEYEVENTF_KEYUP)
  if isExtended(code):
    inp.ki.dwFlags = inp.ki.dwFlags or DWORD(KEYEVENTF_EXTENDEDKEY)
  sendInput(inp, "inject key")

proc sendMouse(dwFlags: DWORD, dx: LONG = 0, dy: LONG = 0, data: DWORD = 0) =
  var inp: INPUT
  inp.`type` = INPUT_MOUSE
  inp.mi.dx = dx
  inp.mi.dy = dy
  inp.mi.mouseData = data
  inp.mi.dwFlags = dwFlags
  sendInput(inp, "inject mouse")

proc injectMouseButton*(button: MouseButton, down: bool) =
  case button
  of mbLeft:
    sendMouse(if down: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN) else: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP))
  of mbRight:
    sendMouse(if down: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN) else: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP))
  of mbMiddle:
    sendMouse(if down: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_MIDDLEDOWN) else: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_MIDDLEUP))
  of mbX1, mbX2:
    let data = if button == mbX1: DWORD(XBUTTON1) else: DWORD(XBUTTON2)
    sendMouse(if down: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_XDOWN) else: DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_XUP), data = data)
  else:
    raise newException(
      OsUnsupported,
      "Windows can inject only left, right, middle, x1 and x2"
    )

proc injectMouseMove*(x, y: int, relative: bool) =
  if relative:
    sendMouse(DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE), LONG(x), LONG(y))
  elif SetCursorPos(x.int32, y.int32) == 0:
    raise inputFailure("move cursor")

proc injectMouseScroll*(amount: float) =
  if amount == 0.0:
    return
  let delta = cast[uint32](int32(round(amount * WHEEL_DELTA.float)))
  sendMouse(DWORD(MOUSEEVENTF_WHEEL), data = DWORD(delta))

proc setCursorPos*(x, y: int) =
  if SetCursorPos(x.int32, y.int32) == 0:
    raise inputFailure("move cursor")

proc focusWindow*(handle: int) =
  let
    hwnd = HWND(handle)
    foreground = GetForegroundWindow()
  if foreground == hwnd:
    return
  let
    foregroundThread =
      if foreground != 0: GetWindowThreadProcessId(foreground, nil) else: 0
    currentThread = GetCurrentThreadId()
  let attached =
    foregroundThread != 0 and
    foregroundThread != currentThread and
    AttachThreadInput(foregroundThread, currentThread, TRUE) != 0
  discard BringWindowToTop(hwnd)
  discard SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)
  discard SetActiveWindow(hwnd)
  discard SetFocus(hwnd)
  if attached:
    discard AttachThreadInput(foregroundThread, currentThread, FALSE)

proc clipCursor*(handle: int) =
  let hwnd = HWND(handle)
  var
    rect: RECT
    topLeft, bottomRight: POINT
  if GetClientRect(hwnd, addr rect) == 0:
    return
  topLeft = POINT(x: rect.left, y: rect.top)
  bottomRight = POINT(x: rect.right, y: rect.bottom)
  if ClientToScreen(hwnd, addr topLeft) == 0 or
    ClientToScreen(hwnd, addr bottomRight) == 0:
    return
  var clip = RECT(
    left: topLeft.x,
    top: topLeft.y,
    right: bottomRight.x,
    bottom: bottomRight.y
  )
  discard ClipCursor(addr clip)

proc releaseCursorClip*() =
  discard ClipCursor(nil)

```

`src/platform/windows.nim`:

```nim
import std/[os, strutils, algorithm]
import winim/lean
import winim/inc/tlhelp32
import winim/inc/wingdi
import common

proc osFailure(op: string, code: int = GetLastError().int): ref OsFailure =
  var msg = ""
  try:
    msg = osErrorMsg(OSErrorCode(code))
  except CatchableError:
    discard
  if msg.len == 0:
    msg = "Windows error " & $code
  result = newException(OsFailure, op & " failed: " & msg)
  result.code = code
  result.denied = code == ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED.int or code == ERROR_PRIVILEGE_NOT_HELD.int

proc permissionHint*(): string =
  "permission denied: the target process may be elevated or protected; " &
  "run pymew from an elevated (administrator) process or disable the " &
  "protection of the target"

proc wcharz(p: pointer): string =
  $cast[WideCString](p)

proc processExists*(pid: int): bool =
  if pid <= 0:
    return false
  let h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, DWORD(pid))
  if h != 0:
    discard CloseHandle(h)
    return true
  result = GetLastError() == ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED

proc openProcessHandle*(pid: int): OsHandle =
  let
    access = DWORD(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION or PROCESS_VM_READ or PROCESS_VM_WRITE or PROCESS_VM_OPERATION)
    h = OpenProcess(access, FALSE, DWORD(pid))
  if h == 0:
    raise osFailure("open process " & $pid)
  result = cast[int](h)

proc closeProcessHandle*(h: OsHandle) =
  if h != 0:
    discard CloseHandle(cast[HANDLE](h))

proc readMemory*(h: OsHandle, address: uint, dst: pointer, size: int): int =
  if size == 0:
    return 0
  var n: SIZE_T
  if ReadProcessMemory(cast[HANDLE](h), cast[LPCVOID](address), dst, SIZE_T(size), addr n) == 0:
    raise osFailure("read at 0x" & toHex(address))
  result = int(n)

proc writeMemory*(h: OsHandle, address: uint, src: pointer, size: int): int =
  if size == 0:
    return 0
  var n: SIZE_T
  if WriteProcessMemory(cast[HANDLE](h), cast[LPVOID](address), src, SIZE_T(size), addr n) == 0:
    raise osFailure("write at 0x" & toHex(address))
  result = int(n)

proc processNameByPid*(pid: int): string =
  let snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0)
  if snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE:
    raise osFailure("snapshot processes")
  defer: discard CloseHandle(snap)
  var pe: PROCESSENTRY32W
  pe.dwSize = DWORD(sizeof(pe))
  var ok = Process32FirstW(snap, addr pe)
  while ok != 0:
    if int(pe.th32ProcessID) == pid:
      return wcharz(addr pe.szExeFile[0])
    ok = Process32NextW(snap, addr pe)
  raise osFailure("find process " & $pid, ERROR_NOT_FOUND.int)

proc enumProcesses*(): seq[RawProcess] =
  let snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0)
  if snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE:
    raise osFailure("snapshot processes")
  defer: discard CloseHandle(snap)
  var pe: PROCESSENTRY32W
  pe.dwSize = DWORD(sizeof(pe))
  var ok = Process32FirstW(snap, addr pe)
  while ok != 0:
    result.add RawProcess(pid: int(pe.th32ProcessID), name: wcharz(addr pe.szExeFile[0]))
    ok = Process32NextW(snap, addr pe)

proc processPath*(pid: int): string =
  let h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, DWORD(pid))
  if h == 0:
    raise osFailure("open process " & $pid)
  defer: discard CloseHandle(h)
  var buf: array[32768, WCHAR]
  var size = DWORD(buf.len)
  if QueryFullProcessImageNameW(h, 0, cast[LPWSTR](addr buf[0]), addr size) == 0:
    raise osFailure("query path of process " & $pid)
  result = wcharz(addr buf[0])

proc processIs64Bit*(pid: int): bool =
  let h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, DWORD(pid))
  if h == 0:
    raise osFailure("open process " & $pid)
  defer: discard CloseHandle(h)
  var wow: WINBOOL
  if IsWow64Process(h, addr wow) == 0:
    raise osFailure("query bitness of process " & $pid)
  result = wow == 0

proc enumModules*(pid: int): seq[RawModule] =
  let snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPMODULE or TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, DWORD(pid))
  if snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE:
    raise osFailure("snapshot modules of process " & $pid)
  defer: discard CloseHandle(snap)
  var me: MODULEENTRY32W
  me.dwSize = DWORD(sizeof(me))
  var ok = Module32FirstW(snap, addr me)
  while ok != 0:
    let
      base = cast[uint](me.modBaseAddr)
      size = uint(me.modBaseSize)
    result.add RawModule(
      name: wcharz(addr me.szModule[0]),
      path: wcharz(addr me.szExePath[0]),
      base: base,
      `end`: base + size,
      size: size
    )
    ok = Module32NextW(snap, addr me)
  result.sort(proc(a, b: RawModule): int = cmp(a.base, b.base))

proc protectionOf(protect, kind: DWORD): string =
  let p = int(protect) and 0xFF
  if p == PAGE_NOACCESS:
    result = "---"
  elif p == PAGE_READONLY:
    result = "r--"
  elif p in [PAGE_READWRITE, PAGE_WRITECOPY]:
    result = "rw-"
  elif p == PAGE_EXECUTE:
    result = "--x"
  elif p == PAGE_EXECUTE_READ:
    result = "r-x"
  elif p in [PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY]:
    result = "rwx"
  else:
    result = "---"
  if kind == MEM_PRIVATE:
    result.add 'p'

proc enumRegions*(pid: int): seq[RawRegion] =
  let h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, FALSE, DWORD(pid))
  if h == 0:
    raise osFailure("open process " & $pid)
  defer: discard CloseHandle(h)
  var address: uint = 0
  var mbi: MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION
  while VirtualQueryEx(h, cast[LPCVOID](address), addr mbi, SIZE_T(sizeof(mbi))) == SIZE_T(sizeof(mbi)):
    let
      base = cast[uint](mbi.BaseAddress)
      size = uint(mbi.RegionSize)
    if size == 0 or base + size <= address:
      break
    if mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT:
      result.add RawRegion(start: base, `end`: base + size, size: size, protection: protectionOf(mbi.Protect, mbi.Type))
    address = base + size

proc processNames*(pid: int): seq[string] =
  var seen: seq[string]
  var names: seq[string]
  try: names.add processNameByPid(pid) except OsFailure: discard
  try: names.add extractFilename(processPath(pid)) except OsFailure: discard
  for n in names:
    if n.len > 0:
      let lower = n.toLowerAscii()
      if lower notin seen:
        seen.add lower
  result = seen

type
  WindowEnumCtx = object
    items: seq[RawWindow]

proc windowTitle(hwnd: HWND): string =
  let length = GetWindowTextLengthW(hwnd)
  if length <= 0:
    return ""
  var buffer = newSeq[WCHAR](length + 1)
  let got = GetWindowTextW(hwnd, cast[LPWSTR](addr buffer[0]), length + 1)
  if got <= 0:
    return ""
  wcharz(addr buffer[0])

proc collectWindow(hwnd: HWND, lparam: LPARAM): WINBOOL {.stdcall.} =
  let ctx = cast[ptr WindowEnumCtx](lparam)
  var pid: DWORD
  discard GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, addr pid)
  if pid == 0:
    return TRUE
  var name = ""
  try:
    name = processNameByPid(int(pid))
  except OsFailure:
    discard
  if name.len == 0:
    return TRUE
  var rect: RECT
  if GetClientRect(hwnd, addr rect) == 0:
    return TRUE
  var origin: POINT
  origin.x = 0
  origin.y = 0
  if ClientToScreen(hwnd, addr origin) == 0:
    return TRUE
  ctx.items.add RawWindow(
    id: int(hwnd),
    pid: int(pid),
    name: name,
    title: windowTitle(hwnd),
    x: int(origin.x),
    y: int(origin.y),
    width: int(rect.right - rect.left),
    height: int(rect.bottom - rect.top),
    visible: IsWindowVisible(hwnd) != 0
  )
  TRUE

proc windowList*(): seq[RawWindow] =
  var ctx: WindowEnumCtx
  discard EnumWindows(collectWindow, cast[LPARAM](addr ctx))
  result = ctx.items

proc findWindow*(pid: int): RawWindow =
  let
    windows = windowList()
    index = pickWindow(windows, pid)
  if index < 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "no window found for process " & $pid)
  result = windows[index]

proc windowGeometry*(id: int): RawWindow =
  let hwnd = cast[HWND](id)
  if IsWindow(hwnd) == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "window " & $id & " no longer exists")
  var rect: RECT
  if GetClientRect(hwnd, addr rect) == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "window " & $id & " no longer exists")
  var origin: POINT
  origin.x = 0
  origin.y = 0
  if ClientToScreen(hwnd, addr origin) == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "window " & $id & " no longer exists")
  RawWindow(
    id: id,
    x: int(origin.x),
    y: int(origin.y),
    width: int(rect.right - rect.left),
    height: int(rect.bottom - rect.top),
    visible: IsWindowVisible(hwnd) != 0
  )

proc screenSize*(): tuple[width, height: int] =
  (
    GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN).int,
    GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN).int
  )

proc collectMonitor(
  monitor: HMONITOR,
  hdc: HDC,
  rect: LPRECT,
  data: LPARAM
): WINBOOL {.stdcall.} =
  let items = cast[ptr seq[RawMonitor]](data)
  var info: MONITORINFOEXW
  info.struct1.cbSize = DWORD(sizeof(MONITORINFOEXW))
  if GetMonitorInfoW(monitor, cast[LPMONITORINFO](addr info)) == 0:
    return TRUE
  var mode: DEVMODEW
  mode.dmSize = WORD(sizeof(DEVMODEW))
  var refresh = 0
  if EnumDisplaySettingsW(cast[LPCWSTR](addr info.szDevice[0]), ENUM_CURRENT_SETTINGS, addr mode) != 0:
    refresh = int(mode.dmDisplayFrequency)
  items[].add RawMonitor(
    name: wcharz(addr info.szDevice[0]),
    x: int(info.struct1.rcMonitor.left),
    y: int(info.struct1.rcMonitor.top),
    width: int(info.struct1.rcMonitor.right - info.struct1.rcMonitor.left),
    height: int(info.struct1.rcMonitor.bottom - info.struct1.rcMonitor.top),
    refresh: refresh
  )
  TRUE

proc monitorList*(): seq[RawMonitor] =
  var items: seq[RawMonitor]
  discard EnumDisplayMonitors(
    cast[HDC](0),
    cast[LPCRECT](nil),
    collectMonitor,
    cast[LPARAM](addr items)
  )
  result = items

proc systemFontDirs*(): seq[string] =
  var dirs: seq[string]
  for root in [getEnv("WINDIR"), getEnv("SystemRoot")]:
    if root.len > 0:
      dirs.add root / "Fonts"
  let localAppData = getEnv("LOCALAPPDATA")
  if localAppData.len > 0:
    dirs.add localAppData / "Microsoft" / "Windows" / "Fonts"
  var seen: seq[string]
  for dir in dirs:
    let key = dir.toLowerAscii()
    if key notin seen:
      seen.add key
      result.add dir
```

`src/platform/x11_hotkey.nim`:

```nim
import std/[os, posix, strutils, typedthreads]
import x11/[x, xlib, xkblib]

import common
import ../keys
import ./x11_input as x11input

const
  maxBindings = 64
  hotkeyModMask = cuint(ShiftMask or ControlMask or Mod1Mask or Mod4Mask)

type
  X11Binding = object
    id: int
    keycode: cint
    mods: uint8
    down: bool

var
  worker: Thread[int]
  display: PDisplay
  rootWindow: Window
  connectionFd: cint
  wakePipe: array[2, cint]
  started: bool
  grabError: cint
  bindings: array[maxBindings, X11Binding]
  bindingCount: int

proc grabErrorHandler(_: PDisplay, event: PXErrorEvent): cint {.cdecl.} =
  if not event.isNil and int(event.error_code) == BadAccess:
    grabError = BadAccess
  0

proc keyModifiers(mods: uint8): cuint =
  if (mods and hmCtrl) != 0:
    result = result or cuint(ControlMask)
  if (mods and hmAlt) != 0:
    result = result or cuint(Mod1Mask)
  if (mods and hmShift) != 0:
    result = result or cuint(ShiftMask)
  if (mods and hmSuper) != 0:
    result = result or cuint(Mod4Mask)

proc grabVariants(base: cuint): array[4, cuint] =
  result[0] = base
  result[1] = base or cuint(LockMask)
  result[2] = base or cuint(Mod2Mask)
  result[3] = base or cuint(LockMask) or cuint(Mod2Mask)

proc ungrabKey(keycode: cint, mods: uint8) =
  for variant in grabVariants(keyModifiers(mods)):
    discard XUngrabKey(display, keycode, variant, rootWindow)

proc grabBinding(op: HotkeyOp): tuple[status: HotkeyStatus, errorCode: int] =
  if bindingCount >= maxBindings:
    return (hsFailure, 0)
  let keycode = XKeysymToKeycode(display, KeySym(keyCodeOs(op.code)))
  if keycode == '\0':
    return (hsUnsupported, 0)
  var binding = X11Binding(id: op.id, keycode: cint(keycode), mods: op.mods)
  let variants = grabVariants(keyModifiers(op.mods))
  discard XSync(display, 0)
  grabError = 0
  let previous = XSetErrorHandler(grabErrorHandler)
  for variant in variants:
    discard XGrabKey(
      display,
      binding.keycode,
      variant,
      rootWindow,
      XBool(0),
      GrabModeAsync,
      GrabModeAsync
    )
  discard XSync(display, 0)
  discard XSetErrorHandler(previous)
  if grabError != 0:
    for variant in variants:
      discard XUngrabKey(display, binding.keycode, variant, rootWindow)
    discard XSync(display, 0)
    return (hsConflict, 0)
  bindings[bindingCount] = binding
  inc bindingCount
  (hsOk, 0)

proc removeBinding(op: HotkeyOp): tuple[status: HotkeyStatus, errorCode: int] =
  for i in 0 ..< bindingCount:
    if bindings[i].id != op.id:
      continue
    ungrabKey(bindings[i].keycode, bindings[i].mods)
    discard XSync(display, 0)
    bindings[i] = bindings[bindingCount - 1]
    dec bindingCount
    return (hsOk, 0)
  (hsFailure, 0)

proc handleEvent(event: var XEvent) =
  if event.theType != KeyPress and event.theType != KeyRelease:
    return
  let
    key = cast[PXKeyEvent](addr event)
    state = cuint(key.state) and hotkeyModMask
  for i in 0 ..< bindingCount:
    if bindings[i].keycode != cint(key.keycode):
      continue
    if keyModifiers(bindings[i].mods) != state:
      continue
    if event.theType == KeyPress:
      if not bindings[i].down:
        bindings[i].down = true
        pushEvent(bindings[i].id)
    else:
      bindings[i].down = false
    return

proc processOps() =
  var op: HotkeyOp
  while takeOp(op):
    case op.kind
    of hoAdd:
      let outcome = grabBinding(op)
      finishOp(outcome.status, outcome.errorCode)
    of hoRemove:
      let outcome = removeBinding(op)
      finishOp(outcome.status, outcome.errorCode)
    of hoStop:
      finishOp(hsOk)

proc hotkeyWorker(_: int) {.thread.} =
  display = XOpenDisplay(nil)
  if display.isNil:
    markWorkerReady(hsUnsupported)
    return
  rootWindow = XDefaultRootWindow(display)
  connectionFd = XConnectionNumber(display)
  discard XkbSetDetectableAutoRepeat(display, true, nil)
  discard XSync(display, 0)
  markWorkerReady(hsOk)
  while true:
    var readSet: TFdSet
    FD_ZERO(readSet)
    FD_SET(connectionFd, readSet)
    FD_SET(wakePipe[0], readSet)
    let highest = max(connectionFd, wakePipe[0])
    if select(highest + 1, addr readSet, nil, nil, nil) < 0:
      continue
    if FD_ISSET(wakePipe[0], readSet) != 0:
      var buffer: array[64, byte]
      discard read(wakePipe[0], addr buffer[0], buffer.len)
      processOps()
    while XPending(display) > 0:
      var event: XEvent
      discard XNextEvent(display, addr event)
      handleEvent(event)

proc hkWake() =
  let byte = 1'u8
  discard write(wakePipe[1], unsafeAddr byte, 1)

proc hkStart*() =
  if started:
    return
  if getEnv("XDG_SESSION_TYPE").toLowerAscii().strip() == "wayland":
    raise newException(
      OsUnsupported,
      "global hotkeys are not available on Wayland; use an X11 session or XWayland"
    )
  initHotkeyState()
  resetWorkerState()
  x11input.ensureXlibThreads()
  if pipe(wakePipe) != 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "cannot create the hotkey wake pipe")
  try:
    createThread(worker, hotkeyWorker, 0)
  except CatchableError:
    discard close(wakePipe[0])
    discard close(wakePipe[1])
    raise
  started = true
  let status = waitWorkerReady()
  if status != hsOk:
    started = false
    discard close(wakePipe[0])
    discard close(wakePipe[1])
    raise newException(
      OsUnsupported,
      "cannot open an X11 display; global hotkeys need an X11 session " &
        "(DISPLAY is not set or the server is unreachable)"
    )

proc hkAdd*(id, code: int, mods: uint8, errorCode: var int): HotkeyStatus =
  hkStart()
  submitOp(HotkeyOp(kind: hoAdd, id: id, code: code, mods: mods), hkWake, errorCode)

proc hkRemove*(id: int, errorCode: var int): HotkeyStatus =
  if not started:
    return hsOk
  submitOp(HotkeyOp(kind: hoRemove, id: id), hkWake, errorCode)

```

`src/platform/x11_input.nim`:

```nim
import std/[os, strutils]
import x11/[x, xlib, xtst, xi2, xinput2]

import common
import ../keys

var
  display: PDisplay
  rootWindow*: Window
  pointerId: cint
  pointerButtons: int
  xlibThreadsReady: bool

proc ensureXlibThreads*() =
  if xlibThreadsReady:
    return
  xlibThreadsReady = true
  discard XInitThreads()

proc openDisplay*(): PDisplay =
  if not display.isNil:
    return display
  ensureXlibThreads()
  if getEnv("XDG_SESSION_TYPE").toLowerAscii().strip() == "wayland":
    raise newException(
      OsUnsupported,
      "X11 is not available on Wayland; global input and window targeting need " &
        "an X11 session or XWayland"
    )
  display = XOpenDisplay(nil)
  if display.isNil:
    raise newException(
      OsUnsupported,
      "cannot open an X11 display; global input and window targeting need an X11 session " &
        "(DISPLAY is not set or the server is unreachable)"
    )
  rootWindow = XDefaultRootWindow(display)
  display

proc buttonNumber(button: MouseButton): int =
  case button
  of mbLeft: 1
  of mbRight: 3
  of mbMiddle: 2
  else: button.int + 4

proc ensurePointer() =
  if pointerId > 0:
    return
  let d = openDisplay()
  var major = cint(2)
  var minor = cint(0)
  if XIQueryVersion(d, addr major, addr minor) != 0:
    raise newException(OsUnsupported, "XInput2 is required to read mouse buttons")
  var count: cint
  let devices = XIQueryDevice(d, XIAllMasterDevices, addr count)
  if devices.isNil:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "cannot query XInput2 devices")
  try:
    let infos = cast[ptr UncheckedArray[XIDeviceInfo]](devices)
    for i in 0 ..< count.int:
      let info = infos[i]
      if info.use != XIMasterPointer:
        continue
      pointerId = info.deviceid
      pointerButtons = 3
      let classes = cast[ptr UncheckedArray[ptr XIAnyClassInfo]](info.classes)
      for j in 0 ..< info.num_classes.int:
        let cls = cast[PXIButtonClassInfo](classes[j])
        if cls.`type` == XIButtonClass:
          pointerButtons = 3 + max(0, cls.num_buttons.int - 7)
          break
      break
  finally:
    XIFreeDeviceInfo(devices)
  if pointerId <= 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "cannot find an XInput2 master pointer")

proc keycodeFor(code: int): KeyCode =
  result = XKeysymToKeycode(openDisplay(), KeySym(keyCodeOs(code)))
  if result == '\0':
    raise newException(OsFailure, "key is not mapped in the current X11 layout")

proc keySet(keys: chararr32, keycode: KeyCode): bool =
  keycode != '\0' and
    (ord(keys[keycode.int div 8]) and (1 shl (keycode.int mod 8))) != 0

proc keyDown*(code: int): bool =
  let
    d = openDisplay()
    keycode = XKeysymToKeycode(d, KeySym(keyCodeOs(code)))
  var keys: chararr32
  if XQueryKeymap(d, keys) == 0:
    return false
  keySet(keys, keycode)

proc keysDown*(): seq[int] =
  let d = openDisplay()
  var keys: chararr32
  if XQueryKeymap(d, keys) == 0:
    return
  for (_, code) in keyTable:
    if keySet(keys, XKeysymToKeycode(d, KeySym(keyCodeOs(code)))):
      result.add code

proc mousePosition*(): tuple[x, y: int] =
  var
    root, child: Window
    rootX, rootY, winX, winY: cint
    mask: cuint
  if XQueryPointer(
    openDisplay(),
    rootWindow,
    addr root,
    addr child,
    addr rootX,
    addr rootY,
    addr winX,
    addr winY,
    addr mask
  ) == 0:
    raise newException(OsFailure, "cannot query the X11 pointer")
  (rootX.int, rootY.int)

proc mouseButtonDown*(button: MouseButton): bool =
  let d = openDisplay()
  ensurePointer()
  var
    root, child: Window
    rootX, rootY, winX, winY: cdouble
    buttons: XIButtonState
    mods: XIModifierState
    group: XIGroupState
  if XIQueryPointer(
    d,
    pointerId,
    rootWindow,
    addr root,
    addr child,
    addr rootX,
    addr rootY,
    addr winX,
    addr winY,
    addr buttons,
    addr mods,
    addr group
  ) == 0:
    discard XFree(buttons.mask)
    raise newException(OsFailure, "cannot query the X11 pointer")
  let
    number = buttonNumber(button)
    index = number div 8
    mask = cast[ptr UncheckedArray[uint8]](buttons.mask)
  result = index < buttons.mask_len.int and
    (mask[index] and (1'u8 shl (number mod 8))) != 0
  discard XFree(buttons.mask)

proc mouseButtonCount*(): int =
  ensurePointer()
  pointerButtons

proc injectKey*(code: int, down: bool) =
  let d = openDisplay()
  discard XTestFakeKeyEvent(d, keycodeFor(code).cuint, XBool(ord(down)), CurrentTime)
  discard XFlush(d)

proc injectMouseButton*(button: MouseButton, down: bool) =
  ensurePointer()
  if button.int > pointerButtons:
    raise newException(ValueError, "the pointer has no button " & $button.int)
  let d = openDisplay()
  discard XTestFakeButtonEvent(d, buttonNumber(button).cuint, XBool(ord(down)), CurrentTime)
  discard XFlush(d)

proc injectMouseMove*(x, y: int, relative: bool) =
  let d = openDisplay()
  if relative:
    discard XTestFakeRelativeMotionEvent(d, x.cint, y.cint, CurrentTime)
  else:
    discard XTestFakeMotionEvent(d, -1, x.cint, y.cint, CurrentTime)
  discard XFlush(d)

proc injectMouseScroll*(amount: float) =
  if amount == 0.0:
    return
  let
    d = openDisplay()
    steps = max(1, int(abs(amount)))
    button = if amount > 0.0: 4'u32 else: 5'u32
  for _ in 0 ..< steps:
    discard XTestFakeButtonEvent(d, button, XBool(1), CurrentTime)
    discard XTestFakeButtonEvent(d, button, XBool(0), CurrentTime)
  discard XFlush(d)

proc setCursorPos*(x, y: int) =
  let d = openDisplay()
  discard XWarpPointer(d, 0, rootWindow, 0, 0, 0, 0, x.cint, y.cint)
  discard XFlush(d)

proc ignoreXError(display: PDisplay, event: PXErrorEvent): cint {.cdecl.} =
  discard display
  discard event
  0

proc focusWindow*(handle: int) =
  if handle == 0:
    return
  let d = openDisplay()
  let previous = XSetErrorHandler(ignoreXError)
  discard XRaiseWindow(d, Window(handle))
  discard XSetInputFocus(d, Window(handle), RevertToPointerRoot, CurrentTime)
  discard XSync(d, XBool(0))
  discard XSetErrorHandler(previous)

proc clipCursor*(handle: int) =
  if handle == 0:
    return
  let d = openDisplay()
  let previous = XSetErrorHandler(ignoreXError)
  discard XGrabPointer(
    d,
    Window(handle),
    XBool(1),
    cuint(ButtonPressMask or ButtonReleaseMask or PointerMotionMask),
    GrabModeAsync,
    GrabModeAsync,
    Window(handle),
    Cursor(0),
    CurrentTime
  )
  discard XSync(d, XBool(0))
  discard XSetErrorHandler(previous)

proc releaseCursorClip*() =
  let d = openDisplay()
  discard XUngrabPointer(d, CurrentTime)
  discard XFlush(d)

```

`src/pyutil.nim`:

```nim
import std/[editdistance, math, strutils]
import macros
import nimpy
import nimpy/[py_types, py_utils, py_lib as pyl]

proc normalizeName*(text: string): string =
  for ch in text:
    if ch notin {'_', '-', ' '}:
      result.add toLowerAscii(ch)

proc clampFactor*(f: float): float =
  if f.isNaN: 0.0 else: clamp(f, 0.0, 1.0)

proc rawPy*(o: PyObject): PPyObject {.inline.} =
  privateRawPyObj(o)

proc closestName*(name: string, candidates: openArray[string], maxDistance: int): string =
  var bestDist = high(int)
  for candidate in candidates:
    let d = editDistance(name, candidate)
    if d < bestDist:
      bestDist = d
      result = candidate
  if bestDist > maxDistance:
    result = ""

proc isNone*(o: PyObject): bool =
  o.isNil or cast[pointer](rawPy(o)) == cast[pointer](pyl.pyLib.Py_None)

proc asPy*(o: PPyObject): PyObject =
  if o.isNil:
    return nil
  var r: PyObject
  pyValueToNim(o, r)
  r

proc argAt*(args: PPyObject, i: int): PyObject =
  if args.isNil or int(pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_Size(args)) <= i:
    return nil
  asPy(cast[PPyObject](pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_GetItem(args, i)))

proc kwArg*(kwargs: PPyObject, name: string): PyObject =
  if kwargs.isNil:
    return nil
  let item = pyl.pyLib.PyDict_GetItemString(kwargs, cstring(name))
  if item.isNil:
    return nil
  asPy(item)

proc takePy*(o: PPyObject): PyObject =
  result = asPy(o)
  decRef o

proc pyResult*(value: PyObject): PPyObject {.inline.} =
  nimValueToPy(value)

proc pyResult*(value: PPyObject): PPyObject {.inline.} =
  value

proc pyResult*[T](value: T): PPyObject =
  when T is void:
    newPyNone()
  else:
    nimValueToPy(value)

proc newPyListOf*(items: openArray[PyObject]): PyObject =
  let l = pyl.pyLib.PyList_New(items.len)
  for i, item in items:
    let raw = rawPy(item)
    incRef raw
    discard pyl.pyLib.PyList_SetItem(l, i, raw)
  result = asPy(l)
  decRef l

proc newPyListTaking*(items: openArray[PPyObject]): PyObject =
  let l = pyl.pyLib.PyList_New(items.len)
  for i, item in items:
    discard pyl.pyLib.PyList_SetItem(l, i, item)
  result = asPy(l)
  decRef l

proc pyIsType*(o: PyObject): bool =
  o.getAttr("__class__") == pyBuiltinsModule().getAttr("type")

proc pyIsInstance*(o: PyObject, name: string): bool =
  callObject(getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "isinstance"), o, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), name)).to(bool)

proc hasAttr*(o: PyObject, name: string): bool =
  let v = pyl.pyLib.PyObject_GetAttrString(rawPy(o), cstring(name))
  if v.isNil:
    pyl.pyLib.PyErr_Clear()
    return false
  decRef v
  result = true

proc tryGetAttr*(o: PyObject, name: string): PyObject =
  let v = pyl.pyLib.PyObject_GetAttrString(rawPy(o), cstring(name))
  if v.isNil:
    pyl.pyLib.PyErr_Clear()
    return nil
  result = asPy(v)
  decRef v

proc setPyAttr*(o: PyObject, name: string, v: PPyObject) =
  let r = pyl.pyLib.PyObject_SetAttrString(rawPy(o), cstring(name), v)
  decRef v
  if r != 0:
    raisePythonError()

proc setPyAttrStr*(o: PyObject, name: string, v: string) =
  setPyAttr(o, name, nimValueToPy(v))

proc setPyAttrInt*(o: PyObject, name: string, v: int) =
  setPyAttr(o, name, nimValueToPy(v))

proc objDict*(o: PyObject): PyObject =
  getAttr(o, "__dict__")

proc dictSet*(d, name, v: PPyObject) =
  if pyl.pyLib.PyDict_SetItem(d, name, v) != 0:
    raisePythonError()

proc dictSetOwned*(d, name, v: PPyObject) =
  dictSet(d, name, v)
  decRef v

var
  keyIsValue: PyObject
  keyAddress: PyObject
  pyTrueValue: PyObject

proc pyMetaKeys() =
  if keyIsValue.isNil:
    keyIsValue = takePy(nimValueToPy("_is_value"))
    keyAddress = takePy(nimValueToPy("_address"))
    pyTrueValue = takePy(nimValueToPy(true))

proc isValueKey*(): PPyObject =
  pyMetaKeys()
  rawPy(keyIsValue)

proc addressKey*(): PPyObject =
  pyMetaKeys()
  rawPy(keyAddress)

proc pyTrue*(): PPyObject =
  pyMetaKeys()
  rawPy(pyTrueValue)

proc isSeq*(o: PyObject): bool =
  let t = cast[ptr PyObjectObj](rawPy(o)).ob_type
  t == cast[pointer](pyl.pyLib.PyList_Type) or t == cast[pointer](pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_Type)

proc seqLen*(o: PyObject): int =
  if cast[ptr PyObjectObj](rawPy(o)).ob_type == cast[pointer](pyl.pyLib.PyList_Type):
    int(pyl.pyLib.PyList_Size(rawPy(o)))
  else:
    int(pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_Size(rawPy(o)))

proc newNamespace*(): PyObject =
  var nsCls {.global.}: PyObject
  if nsCls.isNil:
    nsCls = getAttr(pyImport("types"), "SimpleNamespace")
  result = callObject(nsCls)

proc initIgnore*(self, args, kwargs: PPyObject): PPyObject {.cdecl.} =
  newPyNone()

proc rawKwFunc*(name: string, f: PyCFunctionWithKeywords): PyObject =
  let
    md = cast[ptr PyMethodDef](alloc0(sizeof(PyMethodDef)))
    cs = cast[cstring](alloc0(name.len + 1))
  if name.len > 0:
    copyMem(cs, unsafeAddr name[0], name.len)
  md.ml_name = cs
  md.ml_meth = f
  md.ml_flags = Py_MLFLAGS_VARARGS or Py_MLFLAGS_KEYWORDS
  let o = pyl.pyLib.PyCFunction_NewEx(md, cast[PPyObject](nil), cast[PPyObject](nil))
  result = asPy(o)
  decRef o

proc newPyTuple*(items: openArray[PyObject]): PyObject =
  let t = pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_New(items.len)
  for i, item in items:
    let raw = rawPy(item)
    incRef raw
    discard pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_SetItem(t, i, raw)
  result = asPy(t)
  decRef t

proc newClass*(name: string, base: PyObject, d: PyObject): PyObject =
  result = callObject(getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "type"), name, newPyTuple([base]), d)
  setPyAttr(result, "__module__", nimValueToPy("pymew"))

proc newClassMulti*(name: string, bases: openArray[PyObject], d: PyObject): PyObject =
  result = callObject(getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "type"), name, newPyTuple(bases), d)
  setPyAttr(result, "__module__", nimValueToPy("pymew"))

proc objectNew*(cls: PyObject): PyObject =
  let o = pyl.pyLib.PyType_GenericNew(cast[PyTypeObject](rawPy(cls)), nil, nil)
  if o.isNil:
    raisePythonError()
  result = asPy(o)
  decRef o

var methodClass: PyObject

proc makeMethod*(fn: PPyObject): PyObject =
  if methodClass.isNil:
    var d = pyDict()
    d["__get__"] = (proc(descr, obj, objtype: PyObject): PyObject =
      callObject(getAttr(pyImport("types"), "MethodType"), getAttr(descr, "fn"), obj))
    methodClass = newClass("_Method", getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "object"), d)
  result = callObject(methodClass)
  incRef fn
  setPyAttr(result, "fn", fn)

var typeMethodClass: PyObject

proc makeTypeMethod*(fn: PPyObject): PyObject =
  if typeMethodClass.isNil:
    var d = pyDict()
    d["__get__"] = (proc(descr, obj, objtype: PyObject): PyObject =
      callObject(getAttr(pyImport("types"), "MethodType"), getAttr(descr, "fn"), objtype))
    typeMethodClass = newClass("_TypeMethod", getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "object"), d)
  result = callObject(typeMethodClass)
  incRef fn
  setPyAttr(result, "fn", fn)

template bindMethod*(d: PyObject, name: string, fn: untyped) =
  d[name] = makeMethod(nimValueToPy(fn))

template bindKwMethod*(d: PyObject, name: string, fn: PyCFunctionWithKeywords) =
  d[name] = makeMethod(rawPy(rawKwFunc(name, fn)))

macro exportAs*(prc: typed, name: static[string]): untyped =
  var p = getImpl(prc)
  p[0] = ident(name)
  if p.params.kind == nnkFormalParams and p.params.len > 1:
    if p.params[1].kind == nnkIdentDefs and p.params[1][1].kind == nnkSym:
      p.params[1][1] = ident($p.params[1][1])
  result = newCall(bindSym"exportpy", p)
```

`src/utils.nim`:

```nim
import std/[monotimes, strutils, times]
import nimpy
import nimpy/py_types
import raylib

import pyutil, errors, mem
import platform/api

pyExportModule("pymew")

const
  osName* = when defined(windows): "windows" else: "linux"

var
  startTime = getMonoTime()
  utilsReady = false
  monitorClass*: PyObject
  windowClass*: PyObject

proc ensureUtilsTypes*() =
  if utilsReady:
    return
  utilsReady = true
  monitorClass = buildRecordClass("Monitor", @["name", "x", "y", "width", "height", "refresh"])
  windowClass = buildRecordClass(
    "Window",
    @["id", "pid", "name", "title", "x", "y", "width", "height"]
  )

proc parseTraceLogLevel*(value: PyObject): TraceLogLevel =
  if isNone(value):
    return TraceLogLevel.Error
  if pyIsInstance(value, "bool"):
    raise newException(PmBadType, "log_level must be a name or a number, not bool")
  if pyIsInstance(value, "int"):
    let level = value.to(int)
    if level < 0 or level > ord(TraceLogLevel.None):
      raise newException(PmValueError, "log_level must be between 0 and 7")
    return TraceLogLevel(level)
  if not pyIsInstance(value, "str"):
    raise newException(PmBadType, "log_level must be a str (name) or an int")
  case normalizeName(value.to(string))
  of "all": TraceLogLevel.All
  of "trace": TraceLogLevel.Trace
  of "debug": TraceLogLevel.Debug
  of "info": TraceLogLevel.Info
  of "warning": TraceLogLevel.Warning
  of "error": TraceLogLevel.Error
  of "fatal": TraceLogLevel.Fatal
  of "none": TraceLogLevel.None
  else:
    raise newException(
      PmValueError,
      "unknown log level '" & value.to(string) & "'; use all, trace, debug, info, " &
        "warning, error, fatal or none"
    )

proc monitorRecord(monitor: RawMonitor): PyObject =
  ensureUtilsTypes()
  makeRecord(monitorClass, [
    ("name", nimValueToPy(monitor.name)),
    ("x", nimValueToPy(monitor.x)),
    ("y", nimValueToPy(monitor.y)),
    ("width", nimValueToPy(monitor.width)),
    ("height", nimValueToPy(monitor.height)),
    ("refresh", nimValueToPy(monitor.refresh))
  ])

proc windowRecord(window: RawWindow): PyObject =
  ensureUtilsTypes()
  makeRecord(windowClass, [
    ("id", nimValueToPy(window.id)),
    ("pid", nimValueToPy(window.pid)),
    ("name", nimValueToPy(window.name)),
    ("title", nimValueToPy(window.title)),
    ("x", nimValueToPy(window.x)),
    ("y", nimValueToPy(window.y)),
    ("width", nimValueToPy(window.width)),
    ("height", nimValueToPy(window.height))
  ])

proc utilsOsName*(): string {.exportpy: "_utilsOsName".} =
  osName

proc utilsScreenSize*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_utilsScreenSize".} =
  pyWrap:
    let size = screenSize()
    newPyTuple([
      takePy(nimValueToPy(size.width)),
      takePy(nimValueToPy(size.height))
    ])

proc utilsMonitors*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_utilsMonitors".} =
  pyWrap:
    var items: seq[PyObject]
    for monitor in monitorList():
      items.add monitorRecord(monitor)
    newPyListOf(items)

proc utilsWindows*(): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_utilsWindows".} =
  pyWrap:
    var items: seq[PyObject]
    for window in windowList():
      items.add windowRecord(window)
    newPyListOf(items)

proc utilsWindowInfo*(target: PyObject): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_utilsWindowInfo".} =
  pyWrap:
    const badType = "target must be an int (pid), a str (process name) or a str (window title)"
    if isNone(target):
      raise newException(PmBadType, badType)
    if pyIsInstance(target, "int"):
      let pid = resolveTargetPid(target)
      try:
        windowRecord(findWindow(pid))
      except OsFailure as e:
        raise newException(
          PmWindowNotFound,
          "no window found for target " & $pid & ": " & e.msg
        )
    elif pyIsInstance(target, "str"):
      let
        windows = windowList()
        text = target.to(string)
        wantProc = normalizeProcName(text)
        wantTitle = normalizeName(text)
      var
        best = -1
        bestRank = high(int)
      for i, window in windows:
        let
          procName = normalizeProcName(window.name)
          title = normalizeName(window.title)
        var rank = high(int)
        if wantProc.len > 0 and procName == wantProc:
          rank = 0
        elif wantTitle.len > 0 and title == wantTitle:
          rank = 1
        elif wantProc.len > 0 and wantProc in procName:
          rank = 2
        elif wantTitle.len > 0 and wantTitle in title:
          rank = 3
        if rank == high(int):
          continue
        if rank < bestRank or
            (rank == bestRank and best >= 0 and betterWindow(window, windows[best])):
          best = i
          bestRank = rank
      if best >= 0:
        windowRecord(windows[best])
      else:
        discard resolveTargetPid(target)
        raise newException(
          PmWindowNotFound,
          "no window found for target '" & text & "'"
        )
    else:
      raise newException(PmBadType, badType)

proc utilsTime*(): float64 {.exportpy: "_utilsTime".} =
  (getMonoTime() - startTime).inMilliseconds.float64 / 1000.0

proc utilsSetLogLevel*(level: PyObject = nil): PPyObject {.exportpy: "_utilsSetLogLevel".} =
  pyWrap:
    setTraceLogLevel(parseTraceLogLevel(level))

```

`src/vec.nim`:

```nim
import std/strutils
import nimpy
import nimpy/[py_types, py_utils, py_lib as pyl]
import raylib as rl
import raymath as rm

import pyutil, errors, mem_types

pyExportModule("pymew")

var
  vec2ClassObj*: PyObject
  vec3ClassObj*: PyObject
  vecClassesReady* = false

const compNames = ["x", "y", "z"]

var compKeys: array[3, PyObject]

proc compKey(i: int): PPyObject =
  if compKeys[i].isNil:
    compKeys[i] = takePy(nimValueToPy(compNames[i]))
  rawPy(compKeys[i])

proc buildVec(cls: PyObject, vals: openArray[float]): PyObject =
  result = objectNew(cls)
  let d = rawPy(objDict(result))
  dictSet(d, isValueKey(), pyTrue())
  for i in 0 ..< vals.len:
    dictSetOwned(d, compKey(i), nimValueToPy(vals[i]))

proc vecNewImpl(self, args, kwargs: PPyObject): PPyObject {.cdecl.} =
  try:
    let
      cls = asPy(cast[PPyObject](pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_GetItem(args, 0)))
      dim = getAttr(cls, "_dim").to(int)
      inst = objectNew(cls)
    setPyAttr(inst, "_is_value", nimValueToPy(true))
    let nargs = int(pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_Size(args))
    for i in 0 ..< dim:
      var v = 0.0
      if i + 1 < nargs:
        v = asPy(cast[PPyObject](pyl.pyLib.PyTuple_GetItem(args, i + 1))).to(float)
      elif not kwargs.isNil:
        let item = pyl.pyLib.PyDict_GetItemString(kwargs, cstring(compNames[i]))
        if not item.isNil:
          v = asPy(item).to(float)
      setPyAttr(inst, compNames[i], nimValueToPy(v))
    result = rawPy(inst)
    incRef result
  except CatchableError as e:
    setPyErr(errorClass, e.msg)
    result = nil

proc comp(self: PyObject, i: int): float =
  getAttr(self, compNames[i].cstring).to(float)

proc vecDimOf(v: PyObject): int =
  getAttr(getAttr(v, "__class__"), "_dim").to(int)

proc makeVecLike(a: PyObject, vals: openArray[float]): PyObject =
  let cls = getAttr(a, "__class__")
  result = buildVec(cls, vals)

proc makeVecLike(a: PyObject, v: rl.Vector2): PyObject =
  makeVecLike(a, [float(v.x), float(v.y)])

proc makeVecLike(a: PyObject, v: rl.Vector3): PyObject =
  makeVecLike(a, [float(v.x), float(v.y), float(v.z)])

proc vec2Of(v: PyObject): rl.Vector2 =
  rl.Vector2(x: comp(v, 0).float32, y: comp(v, 1).float32)

proc vec3Of(v: PyObject): rl.Vector3 =
  rl.Vector3(x: comp(v, 0).float32, y: comp(v, 1).float32, z: comp(v, 2).float32)

proc addImpl(a, b: PyObject): PyObject =
  if vecDimOf(a) == 2:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.add(vec2Of(a), vec2Of(b)))
  else:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.add(vec3Of(a), vec3Of(b)))

proc subImpl(a, b: PyObject): PyObject =
  if vecDimOf(a) == 2:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.subtract(vec2Of(a), vec2Of(b)))
  else:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.subtract(vec3Of(a), vec3Of(b)))

proc isNumber(o: PyObject): bool =
  pyIsInstance(o, "int") or pyIsInstance(o, "float")

proc mulImpl(a, b: PyObject): PyObject =
  if isNumber(b):
    let s = b.to(float).float32
    if vecDimOf(a) == 2:
      makeVecLike(a, rm.scale(vec2Of(a), s))
    else:
      makeVecLike(a, rm.scale(vec3Of(a), s))
  elif vecDimOf(a) == 2:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.multiply(vec2Of(a), vec2Of(b)))
  else:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.multiply(vec3Of(a), vec3Of(b)))

proc divImpl(a, b: PyObject): PyObject =
  let s = 1.0'f32 / b.to(float).float32
  if vecDimOf(a) == 2:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.scale(vec2Of(a), s))
  else:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.scale(vec3Of(a), s))

proc negImpl(a: PyObject): PyObject =
  if vecDimOf(a) == 2:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.negate(vec2Of(a)))
  else:
    makeVecLike(a, rm.negate(vec3Of(a)))

proc eqImpl(a, b: PyObject): bool =
  let d = vecDimOf(a)
  if vecDimOf(b) != d:
    return false
  if d == 2:
    rm.equals(vec2Of(a), vec2Of(b)) != 0
  else:
    rm.equals(vec3Of(a), vec3Of(b)) != 0

proc lengthSqImpl(v: PyObject): float =
  if vecDimOf(v) == 2:
    float(rm.lengthSqr(vec2Of(v)))
  else:
    float(rm.lengthSqr(vec3Of(v)))

proc lengthImpl(v: PyObject): float =
  if vecDimOf(v) == 2:
    float(rm.length(vec2Of(v)))
  else:
    float(rm.length(vec3Of(v)))

proc distanceImpl(a, b: PyObject): float =
  if vecDimOf(a) == 2:
    float(rm.distance(vec2Of(a), vec2Of(b)))
  else:
    float(rm.distance(vec3Of(a), vec3Of(b)))

proc normalizedImpl(v: PyObject): PyObject =
  if vecDimOf(v) == 2:
    makeVecLike(v, rm.normalize(vec2Of(v)))
  else:
    makeVecLike(v, rm.normalize(vec3Of(v)))

proc toTupleImpl(v: PyObject): PyObject =
  var items: seq[PyObject]
  for i in 0 ..< vecDimOf(v):
    items.add takePy(nimValueToPy(comp(v, i)))
  newPyTuple(items)

proc iterImpl(v: PyObject): PyObject =
  callObject(getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "iter"), toTupleImpl(v))

proc closestImpl(v, others: PyObject): PyObject =
  var best: PyObject
  var bestDist = Inf
  for raw in rawItems(rawPy(others)):
    let
      o = asPy(raw)
      dist = distanceImpl(v, o)
    if best.isNil or dist < bestDist:
      best = o
      bestDist = dist
  if best.isNil:
    raise newException(ValueError, "closest() requires a non-empty sequence")
  result = best

proc reprImpl(self: PyObject): string =
  let d = vecDimOf(self)
  var parts: seq[string]
  for i in 0 ..< d:
    parts.add $comp(self, i)
  result = "vec" & $d & "(" & parts.join(", ") & ")"

proc makeVecClass(dim: int): PyObject =
  var d = pyDict()
  d["__new__"] = rawKwFunc("__new__", vecNewImpl)
  d["__init__"] = rawKwFunc("__init__", initIgnore)

  bindMethod(d, "__add__", proc(a, b: PyObject): PyObject = addImpl(a, b))
  bindMethod(d, "__sub__", proc(a, b: PyObject): PyObject = subImpl(a, b))
  bindMethod(d, "__mul__", proc(a, b: PyObject): PyObject = mulImpl(a, b))
  bindMethod(d, "__truediv__", proc(a, b: PyObject): PyObject = divImpl(a, b))
  bindMethod(d, "__neg__", proc(a: PyObject): PyObject = negImpl(a))
  bindMethod(d, "__eq__", proc(a, b: PyObject): bool = eqImpl(a, b))
  bindMethod(d, "__iter__", proc(a: PyObject): PyObject = iterImpl(a))
  bindMethod(d, "__repr__", proc(a: PyObject): string = reprImpl(a))

  bindMethod(d, "length", proc(a: PyObject): float = lengthImpl(a))
  bindMethod(d, "length_sq", proc(a: PyObject): float = lengthSqImpl(a))
  bindMethod(d, "distance", proc(a, b: PyObject): float = distanceImpl(a, b))
  bindMethod(d, "normalized", proc(a: PyObject): PyObject = normalizedImpl(a))
  bindMethod(d, "to_tuple", proc(a: PyObject): PyObject = toTupleImpl(a))
  bindMethod(d, "closest", proc(a, b: PyObject): PyObject = closestImpl(a, b))

  result = newClass("vec" & $dim, getAttr(pyBuiltinsModule(), "object"), d)
  setPyAttrInt(result, "_dim", dim)
  let tc = if dim == 2: tcVec2 else: tcVec3
  setPyAttrInt(result, "_code", ord(tc))
  setPyAttrInt(result, "_size", ScalarSizes[tc])

proc ensureVecClasses*() =
  if vecClassesReady:
    return
  vec2ClassObj = makeVecClass(2)
  vec3ClassObj = makeVecClass(3)
  vecClassesReady = true

proc newVec2*(v: rl.Vector2): PyObject =
  ensureVecClasses()
  buildVec(vec2ClassObj, [float(v.x), float(v.y)])

proc newVec2*(x, y: float): PyObject =
  newVec2(rl.Vector2(x: x.float32, y: y.float32))

proc newVec3*(v: rl.Vector3): PyObject =
  ensureVecClasses()
  buildVec(vec3ClassObj, [float(v.x), float(v.y), float(v.z)])

proc newVec3*(x, y, z: float): PyObject =
  newVec3(rl.Vector3(x: x.float32, y: y.float32, z: z.float32))

proc vec2*(x: float = 0.0, y: float = 0.0): PyObject {.exportpy.} =
  newVec2(x, y)

proc vec3*(x: float = 0.0, y: float = 0.0, z: float = 0.0): PyObject {.exportpy.} =
  newVec3(x, y, z)

```

`src/version.nim`:

```nim
import std/strutils

func parseVersion(nimble: string): string =
  for line in nimble.splitLines():
    let entry = line.split('=')
    if entry.len == 2 and entry[0].strip() == "version":
      result = entry[1].strip(chars = {' ', '"'})
      break
  doAssert result.len > 0, "pymew.nimble has no version"

const pymewVersion* = parseVersion(staticRead("../pymew.nimble"))
```
